  1. [2609.25142] 符号为 (n-3,3) 的伪黎曼测地轨道幂零流形
    Pseudo-Riemannian geodesic orbit nilmanifolds of signature $\boldsymbol{(n-3,3)}$
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究指标为3的伪黎曼测地轨道幂零流形，推广洛伦兹和超洛伦兹的结构结果。
    • 假设度量在导代数上非退化，证明幂零代数至多4步，且3步不可能。
    • 4步情形被限制在导代数为洛伦兹且正交补指标为二，并存在不变全迷向二维平面。
    • 给出9维例子证明尖锐性，并讨论退化情形下的双扩张约化。
    💡 研究指标为3的伪黎曼测地轨道幂零流形，证明当度量在导代数上非退化时，幂零代数至多4步，并给出9维例子说明尖锐性。
    📖 中文摘要

    测地轨道性质在黎曼几何中是有用且有趣的，它蕴含齐性，并包含弱对称黎曼流形和自然约化黎曼流形等重要特例。对于不定度量流形，相应结果比黎曼情形微妙得多，但近年来对测地轨道洛伦兹和超洛伦兹流形已证明了重要的结构结果。本文研究指标为三的伪黎曼测地轨道幂零流形，即符号为 (n-3,3) 且 G 的幂零解析子群在 M 上传递的测地轨道伪黎曼流形 M=G/H。假设存在约化分解 g=h⊕n（向量空间直和），其中 n 幂零。当度量在 [n,n] 上非退化时，我们证明 n 是阿贝尔的、2步或4步幂零的。与黎曼、洛伦兹和超洛伦兹情形相反，两步结论失效，但3步幂零仍不可能。4步情形仅限于导代数洛伦兹且正交补指标为二的情况，其结构迫使该补中存在不变的全迷向二维平面。一个显式的9维符号 (6,3) 例子证明了尖锐性。当度量在 [n,n] 上退化时，我们证明存在一个不变迷向子空间，它中心化其正交补，从而给出到严格更小指标的测地轨道度量幂零李代数的双扩张约化。一个8维例子表明这种相对中心性不必蕴含在整个李代数中的中心性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The geodesic orbit property is useful and interesting in Riemannian geometry. It implies homogeneity and has important classes of Riemannian manifolds as special cases, such as weakly symmetric Riemannian manifolds and naturally reductive Riemannian manifolds. The corresponding results for indefinite metric manifolds are much more delicate than in Riemannian signature, but in the last few years important corresponding structural results were proved for geodesic orbit Lorentz and trans-Lorentz manifolds. %Here we carry out a major step in the structural analysis of geodesic orbit Lorentz nilmanifolds. Here we study pseudo-Riemannian geodesic orbit nilmanifolds of metric index three. Those are the geodesic orbit pseudo-Riemannian manifolds $M = G/H$ of signature $(n-3,3)$ such that a nilpotent analytic subgroup of $G$ is transitive on $M$. Suppose that there is a reductive decomposition $\g = \h \oplus \n$ (vector space direct sum) with $\n$ nilpotent. When the metric is nondegenerate on $[\n,\n]$ we show that $\n$ is abelian, $2$-step or $4$-step nilpotent. In contrast to the Riemannian, Lorentzian, and trans-Lorentz cases, the two-step conclusion therefore fails, but $3$-step nilpotency remains impossible. The $4$-step case is confined to a Lorentzian derived algebra with an orthogonal complement of index two, and its structure forces an invariant totally isotropic two-plane in that complement. An explicit nine-dimensional example of signature $(6,3)$ proves sharpness. When the metric is degenerate on $[\n,\n]$ we prove the existence of an invariant isotropic subspace that centralizes its orthogonal complement, yielding a double-extension reduction to a geodesic orbit metric nilpotent Lie algebra of strictly smaller index. An eight-dimensional example shows that this relative centrality need not imply centrality in the full Lie algebra.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25142
  2. [2609.25250] Z-极值Kähler度量的粘合构造与Bismut Hermitian-Einstein 3-流形的新例子
    A gluing construction of $Z$-extremal K\"ahler metrics and new examples of Bismut Hermitian-Einstein $3$-folds
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Kähler流形上六阶几何非线性PDE的粘合构造，该PDE与Bismut Hermitian-Einstein 3-流形和Z-临界Kähler度量相关。
    • 通过粘合构造证明orbifold去奇异化和点爆破定理。
    • 得到多个光滑流形上正则Bismut Hermitian-Einstein结构的新例子。
    • 为超对称AdS_3×Y_7和AdS_2×T^2×Y^7超引力模型提供新解。
    💡 提出Kähler流形上六阶几何非线性PDE的粘合构造，用于构造Bismut Hermitian-Einstein 3-流形及超引力解的新例子。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们为Kähler流形或orbifold上的一类六阶几何非线性偏微分方程提出了粘合构造，这些方程出现在Bismut Hermitian-Einstein复3-流形、物理学中IIB型和D=11超引力模型的超对称 AdS_3×Y_7 和 AdS_2×Y_9 解以及Dervan的Z-临界Kähler度量的研究中。作为应用，我们得到了具有crepant奇点的orbifold在承认该PDE解时的去奇异化定理，以及光滑复3-流形上承认此类解时的点爆破定理。这些结果产生了光滑流形 3(S^2×S^4)#4(S^3×S^3)、5(S^2×S^4)#6(S^3×S^3) 和 S^1×9(S^2×S^3) 上正则Bismut Hermitian-Einstein结构的新例子，以及无限拓扑类型的7维光滑紧流形 Y_7，为超对称 AdS_3×Y_7 型IIB和 AdS_2×T^2×Y^7 型D=11超引力模型提供了新解。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We present a gluing construction for a class of 6th order geometric non-linear PDE's on a K\"ahler manifold or orbifold, arising in the study of Bismut Hermitian Einstein complex $3$-folds, the supersymmetric ${\rm AdS}_3 \times Y_7$ and ${\rm AdS}_2 \times Y_9$ solutions of type IIB and $D=11$ supergravity models in physics, and in Dervan's Z-critical K\"ahler metrics. As applications, we obtain a desingularization theorem for orbifolds with crepant singularities admitting a solution of the PDE, as well as a blowing-up of points theorem for smooth complex $3$-folds admitting such solutions. These results yield new examples of regular Bismut Hermitian Einstein structures on the smooth manifolds $3(S^2\times S^4)\sharp 4(S^3\times S^3)$, $5(S^2\times S^4)\sharp 6(S^3\times S^3)$ and $S^1\times 9(S^2\times S^3)$, as well as infinite topological types of smooth compact $7$-dimensional manifolds $Y_7$, providing new solutions to the supersymmetric ${\rm AdS}_3 \times Y_7$ type IIB and ${\rm AdS}_2 \times T^2 \times Y^7$ type $D=11$ supergravity models.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25250
  3. [2609.25268] 具有势的lcK流形上的Futaki不变量
    Futaki invariant on lcK manifolds with potential
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究具有势的紧局部共形Kähler流形上的Futaki不变量。
    • 证明该不变量恒为零，回答了Ornea和Verbitsky的问题。
    • 这一结果推广了Kähler情形下的经典结论。
    💡 证明Futaki-Hattori-Ornea引入的Futaki不变量在具有势的紧局部共形Kähler流形上恒为零，回答了Ornea和Verbitsky的问题。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明由Futaki、Hattori和Ornea引入的Futaki不变量在每个具有势的紧局部共形Kähler流形上恒为零，从而回答了Ornea和Verbitsky提出的一个问题。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that the Futaki invariant introduced by Futaki, Hattori, and Ornea vanishes identically on every compact locally conformally K\"ahler manifold with potential, thereby answering a question raised by Ornea and Verbitsky.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25268
  4. [2609.25289] 漂移拉普拉斯算子的两个增长滤链：相容性、刚性及Hermite/Laguerre逆定理
    Two Growth Filtrations for Drift Laplacians: Compatibility, Rigidity, and an Inverse Hermite/Laguerre Theorem
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究非紧黎曼流形上漂移拉普拉斯算子特征函数的增长滤链与特征值滤链的相容性。
    • 在多项式界和增长阶假设下，研究关系 λ_k≍γ_k^{(2α-2)/α}，并给出反例说明一般情形失效。
    • 在warped乘积或传递等距对称性假设下，证明 α=2 且 λ_k≍γ_k≍k。
    • 不变特征函数的精确多项式性导出Hermite或Laguerre方程，且论证不依赖曲率界。
    💡 研究非紧黎曼流形上漂移拉普拉斯算子的特征函数增长滤链与特征值滤链的相容性，在对称性假设下证明λ_k≍γ_k≍k，并导出Hermite或Laguerre方程。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 (M^n,g) 为完备连通非紧黎曼流形，f≥0 为真 C^2 权函数。我们假设 f、|∇f| 和 Δf 有多项式界（假设H1-H3），增长指数 α>0。我们研究 L^2(M,e^{-f}dV) 上的漂移拉普拉斯算子 L_f=Δ-⟨∇f,∇·⟩。假设其特征函数在 f^{1/α} 的水平集上具有有限多项式增长阶 γ_k，且 γ_k 按谱序趋于无穷（假设H4）。我们将此增长滤链与特征值 λ_k 的滤链进行比较。对于 α>1，我们研究关系 λ_k≍γ_k^{(2α-2)/α}，称为相容性。一个显式的旋转对称例子表明，即使 α=2，该关系对全谱也可能失效。我们证明 α>1 时的离散性和加权Agmon估计。一个矩估计用增长阶和有限尺度前因子来界定集中半径。一个下局部化条件给出谱比较的一个方向。对于具有一维基的精确warped乘积，我们施加径向增长条件和径向漂移的正则性假设，得到 α=2 且径向部分 λ_k≍γ_k。对于一般 (M,g)，我们假设水平集具有传递等距对称性而非warped乘积结构。不变部分增长条件和径向漂移正则性给出 α=2 且 λ_k≍γ_k≍k。最后，不变特征函数的精确多项式性蕴含 α=2，无需漂移正则性假设。归一化后约化方程为Hermite或广义Laguerre方程。论证不使用曲率界或孤子方程。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $(M^n,g)$ be a complete, connected, noncompact Riemannian manifold, and let $f\ge0$ be a proper $C^2$ weight. We assume polynomial bounds on $f$, $|\nabla f|$, and $\Delta f$ (Assumptions H1-H3), with growth exponent $\alpha>0$. We study the drift Laplacian $L_f=\Delta-\langle\nabla f,\nabla\cdot\rangle$ on $L^2(M,e^{-f}dV)$. We assume that its eigenfunctions have finite polynomial growth orders $\gamma_k$, measured on the level sets of $f^{1/\alpha}$. We also assume that $\gamma_k\to\infty$ in spectral order (Assumption H4). We compare this growth filtration with the filtration by eigenvalue $\lambda_k$. For $\alpha>1$, we study the relation $\lambda_k\asymp\gamma_k^{(2\alpha-2)/\alpha}$, called compatibility. An explicit rotationally symmetric example shows that this relation can fail for the full spectrum, even when $\alpha=2$. We prove discreteness and weighted Agmon estimates for $\alpha>1$. A moment estimate bounds the concentration radius in terms of the growth order and a finite-scale prefactor. A lower localization condition gives one direction of the spectral comparison. For exact warped products with a one-dimensional base, we impose the radial growth condition and a regularity assumption on the radial drift. These give $\alpha=2$ and $\lambda_k\asymp\gamma_k$ in the radial sector. For general $(M,g)$, we assume a transitive isometric symmetry of the level sets instead of a warped-product structure. The invariant-sector growth condition and radial-drift regularity then give $\alpha=2$ and $\lambda_k\asymp\gamma_k\asymp k$. Finally, exact polynomiality of the invariant eigenfunctions implies $\alpha=2$ without the drift regularity assumptions. The reduced equation is then Hermite or generalized Laguerre after normalization. The arguments use neither curvature bounds nor soliton equations.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25289
  5. [2609.25309] Calabi-Yau锥的crepant消解上的dHYM方程
    The dHYM equation on crepant resolutions of Calabi-Yau cones
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究非紧Calabi-Yau流形 Z=Tot(K_X) 上的dHYM方程，其中 X 是有理齐性簇。
    • 利用余齐一对称性将完全非线性PDE约化为标量ODE，得到全局解存在的精确相位条件。
    • 证明每个全纯线丛在相位位于显式区间时承认dHYM解，并给出坍缩为HYM联络的刚性分类。
    • 构造了非环面Calabi-Yau流形上第一个非dHYM的Hermitian-Einstein联络。
    💡 研究非紧Calabi-Yau流形上dHYM方程，将PDE约化为常微分方程，给出全局解存在的精确相位条件，并构造非环面Calabi-Yau上非dHYM的Hermitian-Einstein联络。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究非紧Calabi-Yau流形 Z=Tot(K_X) 上的变形Hermitian-Yang-Mills (dHYM) 方程，其中 X 是有理齐性簇。由于 X 是Fano簇，Z 是仿射锥 Aff(X) 顶点奇点的消解，前提是锥由反规范极化 L=K_X^{-1} 构建。利用 Z 上由Calabi ansatz得到的Ricci平坦Kähler度量的余齐一对称性，我们将dHYM方程背后的完全非线性PDE约化为一个标量的渐近自治常微分方程(ODE)。由此，我们确定了保证全局解存在的拓扑相位的精确条件。作为应用，我们证明 Z 上每个全纯线丛都承认一个光滑的全局Hermitian联络解dHYM方程，只要总相位位于由Lie理论数据确定的显式开区间内。此外，我们证明了一个刚性结果，分类了dHYM解坍缩为经典Hermitian-Yang-Mills (HYM) 联络的精确几何条件。所得结果推广了先前的构造，并提供了一大类新例子。此外，所提出的方法允许通过ODE方法研究dHYM解的行为。利用此方法，我们构造了非环面Calabi-Yau流形上线丛的第一个显式非平凡Hermitian-Einstein联络，该联络不是dHYM的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we study the deformed Hermitian-Yang-Mills (dHYM) equation on the non-compact Calabi-Yau manifold $Z = {\rm{Tot}}({\bf{K}}_{X})$, where $X$ is a rational homogeneous variety. Since $X$ is a Fano variety, $Z$ is a resolution of the singularity at the vertex of the affine cone ${\rm{Aff}}(X)$, provided the cone is built using the anticanonical polarization ${\bf{L}} = {\bf{K}}_{X}^{-1}$. Using the cohomogeneity-one symmetry of the Ricci-flat K\"{a}hler metric obtained via the Calabi ansatz on $Z$, we reduce the fully nonlinear PDE underlying the dHYM equation to a scalar, asymptotically autonomous ordinary differential equation (ODE). From this, we determine the exact condition on the topological phase that guarantees global existence of solutions. As an application, we show that every holomorphic line bundle over $Z$ admits a smooth, globally defined Hermitian connection solving the dHYM equation, provided the total phase lies in an explicit open interval determined by the Lie-theoretic data. Also, we prove a rigidity result classifying the exact geometric conditions under which the dHYM solution collapses into a classical Hermitian-Yang-Mills (HYM) connection. The results established generalize previous constructions and provide a substantial new class of examples. Furthermore, the approach presented allows one to study the behavior of the dHYM solutions through ODE methods. Using this approach, we construct the first explicit non-trivial example of a Hermitian-Einstein connection on a line bundle over a non-toric Calabi-Yau manifold which is not dHYM.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25309
  6. [2609.25550] 欧几里得空间扰动的连续正质量定理
    A continuous Positive Mass Theorem for perturbations of Euclidean space
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究C^0渐近平坦度量在弱非负标量曲率下的正质量定理。
    • 证明若度量是欧几里得空间的C^0扰动且在Ricci流意义下非负标量曲率，则其质量非负。
    • 质量定义为经典ADM质量的C^0类似物。
    💡 证明在Ricci流意义下非负标量曲率的C^0渐近平坦度量若充分接近欧几里得空间，则其质量非负。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明了一个正质量定理，适用于在弱意义下具有非负标量曲率且充分一致接近欧几里得空间的 C^0 渐近平坦黎曼度量。更精确地说，我们证明一个 C^0 渐近平坦黎曼度量，若它是欧几里得空间的 C^0 扰动且在Ricci流意义下具有非负标量曲率，则其质量非负，其中质量由作者先前引入的经典ADM质量的 C^0 类似物给出。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove a Positive Mass Theorem for $C^0$-asymptotically flat Riemannian metrics with nonnegative scalar curvature in a weak sense that are sufficiently uniformly close to Euclidean space. More precisely, we show that a $C^0$-asymptotically flat Riemannian metric that is a $C^0$ perturbation of Euclidean space with nonnegative scalar curvature in the sense of Ricci flow has nonnegative mass, where the mass is given by a $C^0$ analog of the classical ADM mass previously introduced by the author.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25550
  7. [2609.25553] 超临界LYZ方程边界情形的除子刚性与正则性
    Divisorial Rigidity and Regularity of Boundary Cases for the Supercritical LYZ Equation
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究紧Kähler流形上超临界LYZ方程在稳定性边界的行为。
    • 在数值半稳定下证明修正nef锥上的定量正性估计，得到 destabilizing 除子的有限例外族。
    • 假设光滑半子解存在，证明相交形式在除子类张成空间上负定。
    • 构造对数奇异子解，得到有界Bedford-Taylor解并在补集上光滑。
    💡 研究紧Kähler流形上超临界LYZ方程在稳定性边界的行为，证明在数值半稳定下 destabilizing 除子形成有限例外族，并构造对数奇异子解得到有界Bedford-Taylor解。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究紧Kähler流形上超临界LYZ方程在稳定性边界的行为。在数值半稳定假设下，我们证明了修正nef锥上的一个定量正性估计。它蕴含 destabilizing 素除子形成一个有限例外族。假设存在光滑半子解，我们证明相关相交形式在这些除子类张成的空间上是负定的。利用此刚性，我们沿它们的并构造了一个对数奇异子解，并得到一个有界Bedford-Taylor解，该解在其补集上光滑。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the supercritical LYZ equation on compact K\"ahler manifolds at the boundary of stability. Under numerical semistability, we prove a quantitative positivity estimate on the modified nef cone. It implies that the destabilizing prime divisors form a finite exceptional family. Assuming the existence of a smooth semisubsolution, we prove that the associated intersection form is negative definite on the span of classes of these divisors. Using this rigidity, we then construct a logarithmic singular subsolution along their union and obtain a bounded Bedford--Taylor solution which is smooth on its complement.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25553
  8. [2609.25676] 时间依赖扰动Hodge-de Rham算子的Hamilton型谱泛函
    Hamiltonian-Type Spectral Functionals for Time-Dependent Perturbed Hodge--de Rham Operators
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究时间依赖扰动Hodge-de Rham算子的Hamilton型谱泛函。
    • 利用系数导数计算标量加权残数泛函，速度贡献仅依赖度量导数。
    • 符号计算将lapse修正约化为散度和Dirichlet项，并保留混合梯度项。
    • 在带warped collar的紧流形上显式计算边界残数，边界为空时恢复闭公式。
    💡 计算时间依赖扰动Hodge-de Rham算子的标量加权残数泛函，将lapse修正约化为散度和Dirichlet项，并在带warped collar的紧流形上显式计算边界残数。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 g_t 是定向偶数维流形 n≥4 上的光滑黎曼度量族，D_t=d+δ_{g_t}+Ψ_t 作用在固定外丛上，其中 Ψ_t 自伴。我们利用系数导数 ∂_tD_t 计算了从Hawkins Hamilton作用中的算子表达式得到的标量加权残数泛函。速度贡献仅依赖于 ∂_tg_t。直接的符号计算将lapse修正约化为一个散度和一个Dirichlet项；对于非常数测试函数，分部积分保留一个混合梯度项。对于带warped collar的紧流形，我们指定了加权KKW项和交换子修正的相容分解。它们的边界残数被显式计算。在加权KKW分解中，collar导数和扰动贡献相消，剩余的边界项涉及标量权的法向导数。当边界为空时，恢复闭公式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $g_t$ be a smooth family of Riemannian metrics on an oriented manifold of even dimension $n\geq4$, and let $D_t=d+\delta_{g_t}+\Psi_t$ on the fixed exterior bundle, with $\Psi_t$ self-adjoint. We evaluate a scalar-weighted residue functional obtained from the operator expression in Hawkins' Hamiltonian action using the coefficient derivative $\partial_tD_t$. The velocity contribution depends only on $\partial_tg_t$. A direct symbol calculation reduces the lapse correction to a divergence and a Dirichlet term; for a nonconstant test function, integration by parts retains a mixed gradient term. For compact manifolds with a warped collar, we specify compatible factorizations of the weighted KKW term and of the commutator correction. Their boundary residues are computed explicitly. The collar-derivative and perturbation contributions cancel in the weighted KKW factorization, and the remaining boundary terms involve normal derivatives of the scalar weights. The closed formula is recovered when the boundary is empty.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明紧Hermitian流形上常Chern全纯截面曲率的两个刚性定理。在Fujiki类 C 中的流形上，每个具有常非正Chern全纯截面曲率的Hermitian度量都是Kähler的，无需平衡或pluriclosed假设。在负情形，流形是射影的且具有丰沛规范丛，给定度量是归一化负Kähler-Einstein度量，具有复双曲万有覆盖。在零情形，度量是平坦的，流形允许复环面的有限étale覆盖。我们还证明常正Chern全纯截面曲率的紧平衡三维流形全纯等距于带缩放Fubini-Study度量的复射影三维空间。非正论证使用Kähler背景上的积分Chern-Lu恒等式。对于负曲率，扭曲Kähler-Einstein体积的下界给出规范丰沛性，加权Stokes恒等式和体积比矩不等式识别给定度量。在零曲率，张量Bochner论证在Ricci平坦背景上给出平行性。对于平衡三维流形，微分相容性和挠率能量恒等式给出强制性估计，通过在每个挠率秩有效的有理矩阵分解证明。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove two rigidity theorems for compact Hermitian manifolds with constant Chern holomorphic sectional curvature. On a manifold in Fujiki's class $\mathcal C$, every Hermitian metric of constant nonpositive Chern holomorphic sectional curvature is K\"ahler, without a balanced or pluriclosed assumption. In the negative case, the manifold is projective with ample canonical bundle, and the given metric is the normalized negative K\"ahler--Einstein metric, with complex hyperbolic universal cover. In the zero case, the metric is flat and the manifold admits a finite \'etale cover by a complex torus. We also prove that a compact balanced threefold of constant positive Chern holomorphic sectional curvature is holomorphically isometric to complex projective three-space with a scaled Fubini--Study metric. The nonpositive argument uses an integrated Chern--Lu identity on a K\"ahler background. For negative curvature, a lower bound for twisted K\"ahler--Einstein volumes yields canonical ampleness, and weighted Stokes identities and volume-ratio moment inequalities identify the given metric. At zero curvature, a tensor Bochner argument gives parallelness on a Ricci-flat background. For balanced threefolds, differential compatibility and the torsion energy identities yield a coercive estimate, proved by a rational matrix decomposition valid at every torsion rank.

    Weakly Einstein and weakly $\eta$-Einstein structures on almost contact metric three-manifolds
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究几乎接触度量三维流形上的弱Einstein和弱η-Einstein结构。
    • 给出Ricci算子的点态结构描述：η-Einstein形式、Qξ=0或弱Einstein。
    • 证明接触度量三维流形中Qξ=0蕴含Q=0或rank Q=1，并构造反例说明接触假设的必要性。
    • 分类单连通齐性接触度量三维流形中的弱Einstein度量。
    💡 研究几乎接触度量三维流形上的弱Einstein和弱η-Einstein结构，给出Ricci算子的点态结构描述，并分类单连通齐性接触度量三维流形中的弱Einstein度量。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究几乎接触度量三维流形上的弱Einstein和弱η-Einstein结构。首先，我们得到弱η-Einstein几乎接触度量三维流形上Ricci算子 Q 的点态结构描述。更精确地说，在每一点，要么 Q 具有η-Einstein形式，要么 Qξ=0，要么度量是弱Einstein的。然后我们证明，如果接触度量三维流形满足 Qξ=0，则要么 Q=0，要么 rank Q=1。因此，这样的度量要么平坦，要么弱Einstein但非Einstein。我们还构造例子表明接触度量假设是本质的：存在满足 Qξ=0 且Ricci算子秩为二的几乎接触度量三维流形。最后，我们分类单连通齐性接触度量三维流形中的弱Einstein度量。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study weakly Einstein and weakly $\eta$-Einstein structures on almost contact metric three-manifolds. We first obtain a pointwise structural description of the Ricci operator $Q$ on a weakly $\eta$-Einstein almost contact metric three-manifold. More precisely, at each point, either $Q$ has the $\eta$-Einstein form, $Q\xi=0$, or the metric is weakly Einstein. We then prove that if a contact metric three-manifold satisfies $Q\xi=0$, then either $Q=0$ or $\operatorname{rank}Q=1$. Hence, such a metric is either flat or weakly Einstein but not Einstein. We also construct examples showing that the contact metric assumption is essential: there exist almost contact metric three-manifolds satisfying $Q\xi=0$ whose Ricci operators have rank two. Finally, we classify simply connected homogeneous contact metric three-manifolds that are weakly Einstein.

    📖 中文摘要

    非Kähler几何中一个长期猜想是：任何具有常全纯截面曲率的紧Hermitian流形必须是Kähler或Chern平坦的。该猜想在二维已知成立，但在三维或更高维仍开放，除了一些特殊情形。本文考虑紧Hermitian流形是Bismut挠率平行（简称BTP）的情形，即Bismut联络具有平行挠率。对于非平衡的BTP流形，Chen和Zheng证明了该猜想。他们还利用Zhao和Zheng关于此类三维流形的分类结果，确认了三维平衡BTP流形的猜想。在最近的工作中，我们通过详细的逐例分析确认了四维平衡BTP流形的猜想。本文中，我们在所有维度上建立了平衡BTP流形的猜想。我们还通过构造一个紧平衡BTP五维流形，其具有全秩 B 且既非Kähler也非Chern平坦，其Chern全纯截面曲率非常数，从而否定了全秩 B 猜想。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A long-lasting conjecture in non-K\"ahler geometry says that any compact Hermitian manifold with constant holomorphic sectional curvature must be either K\"ahler or Chern flat. The conjecture is known to be true in dimension 2 but remains open in dimensions 3 or higher, except in several special cases. In this article we consider compact Hermitian manifolds that are Bismut torsion-parallel (BTP for brevity), which means that the Bismut connection has parallel torsion. For BTP manifolds that are not balanced, the conjecture was proved by Chen and Zheng. They also confirmed the conjecture for balanced BTP manifolds in dimension 3, utilizing a classification result for such threefolds by Zhao and Zheng. In a recent work, we confirmed the conjecture for balanced BTP manifolds in dimension 4 through a detailed case-by-case analysis. In this article, we establish the conjecture for balanced BTP manifolds in all dimensions. We also disprove the full-rank $B$ conjecture by constructing a compact balanced BTP fivefold with full-rank $B$ that is neither K\"ahler nor Chern flat; its Chern holomorphic sectional curvature is nonconstant.

    我们研究LVM流形商构造的四元数对应。从容许配置 Λ=(λ_1,…,λ_n)⊂H^m≃R^{4m}，n>4m，我们得到实二次曲面的光滑紧交 Z_H(Λ)⊂H^n，其 Sp(1)^n 轨道空间是简单凸多面体。正实坐标伸缩的典范作用以 Z_H(Λ) 为全局归一化横截面，投影化后给出紧商 N_H(Λ)=Z_H(Λ)/Sp(1)，我们称之为LVMQ流形。全空间是四元数矩角多面体积 (D^4,S^3)^{K_{P_Λ}}。每个LVMQ流形是2-连通的；在没有不可或缺坐标时是3-连通的，且 H^4(N_H(Λ);Z)≅Z，p_1(TN_H(Λ))=2(n-2)e。这里 p_1 表示第一Pontryagin类，e 是主 Sp(1)-丛 Z_H(Λ)→N_H(Λ) 的Euler类。对于显式无限球乘积族，环境Kraines形式限制为4-辛形式，所得LVMQ流形在Gentili-Gori-Sarfatti意义下是四元数环面的。我们还研究互补Poincaré域及其迹叶状结构。对于四元数秩一问题，可逆线性场的Frobenius可积性迫使标量Hopf模型，而小的非线性可积扰动给出到同伦等价于四元数射影空间的光滑流形上的Hopf型 S^3-纤维化。

We also study the complementary Poincar\'e domain and its trace foliations. In dimension three, we prove that the CR Paneitz operator is bounded below without assuming embeddability. The proofs combine the Heisenberg calculus with a square-root construction based on the joint functional calculus of the sub-Laplacian and the Reeb vector field on the Heisenberg group. As an application, we show that the infinitely many negative eigenvalues of the CR Paneitz operator on the Rossi sphere have zero as their only accumulation point. Consequently, this operator does not have closed range.

    🌐 English Abstract

The proof uses an almost convex smooth exhaustion together with a separate smooth approximation to the distance function. The negative curvature errors of the level sets are integrable. In the two-ended case the splitting theorem gives an actual limit bounded by $16\pi$. The one-ended bound is attained for every prescribed asymptotic volume ratio in $[0,1]$; the two-ended constant is also attained. The one-ended conclusion is an upper-limit bound; existence of the corresponding limit is not asserted.

    📖 中文摘要

To prove this, we introduce a $2$-parameter ``canonical family" associated to any non-orientable surface in $\mathbb{S}^3$ with boundary a great circle and apply a min-max argument. The canonical family detects the Euler number of the surface and is inspired by the $5$-parameter family discovered by F.C. Marques and A. Neves detecting the genus of an orientable surface in $\mathbb{S}^3$. For $\mathbb{Z}_2$-invariant Klein bottles, this reduces R. Kusner's 1989 conjecture that $\tau_{1,2}$ minimizes the Willmore energy for a Klein bottle immersed in $\mathbb{S}^3$ to any of several conjectural characterizations of the Lawson M\"obius band.

We then formulate a general Euler-Gysin realization theorem using relative Fredholm bundles and determinant-line sewing. For phase trivial Calabi-Yau surfaces we verify the admissibility conditions directly and obtain a canonically defined associative graded cohomology algebra containing the Chen-Hu even sector and the odd fixed surfaces.     🌐 English Abstract

The resulting parabolic--elliptic system incorporates renormalization into the evolution while leaving the Dirichlet energy functional and its harmonic critical points unchanged. We establish local well-posedness for $H^3$ initial data and a continuation criterion in terms of energy concentration. For sufficiently large coupling, we exclude finite-time bubbling and obtain global solutions. At infinite time, the energy identity provides only weighted $L^2$ control of the tension field. Localizing to regular regions of concentration charts yields harmonic profiles along subsequences of prescribed times.     📖 中文摘要

    等变卷积神经网络通常由全局作用于信号空间的群构建。这一假设对于许多有界或分层域是不合适的：环境刚体运动可能仅在域的一部分上允许，而边界引入了传递群作用不可见的几何类型。我们发展群胚等变神经网络理论，其中对称性数据由群胚、选定的局部双截面的伪群、测度以及输入输出表示丛组成。对于对象空间上的积分通道，我们证明双截面等变核定理：等变性等价于两点核上的传输约束，其解由每对轨道上的一个联合稳定化子 intertwiner 分类。作为案例研究，我们将理论应用于有界平面域。所得架构通过离线零空间基和稀疏gather-transform-scatter操作实现。Poisson-Dirichlet核研究用于评估边界感知归纳偏差；精确逆保持矩形的全局对称性但不保持一般真局部双截面。数值结果表明，当对称性不能由群作用全局实现时，所提架构具有显著优势，并且相对于测试模型精度提高至少一个数量级。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Equivariant convolutional neural networks are usually built from a group acting globally on the space of signals. This hypothesis is inappropriate for many bounded or stratified domains: an ambient rigid motion may be admissible only on part of the domain, and the boundary introduces geometric types that are invisible to a transitive group action. We develop a theory of groupoid-equivariant neural networks in which the symmetry datum consists of a groupoid, a selected pseudogroup of local bisections, a measure, and input and output representation bundles. For integral channels on the object space, we prove a bisection-equivariant kernel theorem: equivariance is equivalent to a transport constraint on the two-point kernel, and its solutions are classified by one joint-stabilizer intertwiner on each orbit of pairs. As a case study we apply the theory to bounded planar domains. The resulting architecture is implemented through offline nullspace bases and sparse gather--transform--scatter operations. A Poisson--Dirichlet kernel study is used separately to assess boundary-aware inductive bias; the exact inverse is shown to preserve the global symmetries of the rectangle but not general proper local bisections. The numerical results show that the proposed architectures provide significant advantages when symmetries cannot be globally implemented by group actions and provide an accuracy improvement of at least one order of magnitude with respect to the models tested.

    • 证明界的尖锐性，并指出极值不可达。
    💡 证明Langford-Laugesen猜想的第三Neumann特征值双圆盘界，并加强为倒数不等式。
    📖 中文摘要

    Langford和Laugesen猜想在高斯曲率上界下第三Neumann特征值的尖锐双圆盘界（Math. Ann. 386 (2023), 2255--2281, Conjecture 1.4）。我们证明了对具有Langford和Laugesen所用权重正则性的有界Lipschitz膜的猜想界，并将其加强为尖锐倒数不等式。设$\Omega\subset\mathbb{C}$是有界单连通Lipschitz域，$\omega\in C^2(\Omega)\cap C(\overline{\Omega})$在$\overline{\Omega}$上为正，$\Omega$配备$g=\omega|dz|^2$。假设$K_g\le K$，$A=\int_\Omega\omega\,dx>0$，且当$K>0$时$KA<4\pi$。按重数枚举Neumann特征值$0=\lambda_0<\lambda_1\le\lambda_2\le\cdots$。若$D_K(A/2)$是面积为$A/2$的常曲率测地圆盘，则$\frac{1}{\lambda_2(\Omega,g)}+\frac{1}{\lambda_3(\Omega,g)}>\frac{2}{\lambda_1(D_K(A/2))}$且$\lambda_2(\Omega,g)<\lambda_1(D_K(A/2))$。不需要$\lambda_1$的单性或$\omega$的边界可微性。相同结论对光滑黎曼曲面中的相对紧圆盘型Lipschitz域成立。两个界在固定面积和曲率上界下是尖锐的：对每个容许的$K,A$，常曲率模型中面积为$A$的光滑连通域序列的固定指标Neumann谱收敛到$D_K(A/2)\sqcup D_K(A/2)$的谱。两个极值在连通类中均不可达。证明使用双极点Green坐标、正核比较、两个复矩的同时中心化和移位倒数变分估计。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Langford and Laugesen conjectured a sharp two-disk bound for the third Neumann eigenvalue under an upper Gaussian-curvature bound (Math. Ann. 386 (2023), 2255--2281, Conjecture 1.4). We prove the conjectured bound for bounded Lipschitz membranes with the weight regularity used by Langford and Laugesen, and strengthen it to a sharp reciprocal inequality. Let $\Omega\subset\mathbb{C}$ be a bounded simply connected Lipschitz domain, let $\omega\in C^2(\Omega)\cap C(\overline{\Omega})$ be positive on $\overline{\Omega}$, and equip $\Omega$ with $g=\omega|dz|^2$. Suppose $K_g\le K$, $A=\int_\Omega\omega\,dx>0$, and $KA<4\pi$ when $K>0$. Enumerate the Neumann eigenvalues, counting multiplicity, by $0=\lambda_0<\lambda_1\le\lambda_2\le\cdots$. If $D_K(A/2)$ is the constant-curvature geodesic disk of area $A/2$, then $\frac{1}{\lambda_2(\Omega,g)}+\frac{1}{\lambda_3(\Omega,g)}>\frac{2}{\lambda_1(D_K(A/2))}$ and $\lambda_2(\Omega,g)<\lambda_1(D_K(A/2))$. No simplicity of $\lambda_1$ or boundary differentiability of $\omega$ is required. The same conclusions hold for relatively compact disk-type Lipschitz domains in smooth Riemannian surfaces. Both bounds are sharp at fixed area and curvature upper bound: for each admissible $K,A$, a sequence of smooth connected domains of area $A$ in the constant-curvature model has fixed-index Neumann spectra converging to those of $D_K(A/2)\sqcup D_K(A/2)$. Neither extremal value is attained in either connected class. The proof uses two-pole Green coordinates, a positive-kernel comparison, simultaneous centering of two complex moments, and a shifted reciprocal variational estimate.

  40. [2609.26768] Bogomolov-Guan Manifolds are not formal
    Bogomolov-Guan Manifolds are not formal
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    Bogomolov--Guan manifolds form the only known series of compact simply connected non-K\"ahler holomorphic symplectic manifolds. We prove that all Bogomolov--Guan manifolds of complex dimension at least four are nonformal over $\mathbb{Q}$. The proof constructs a uniform degree-eight $a$-Massey product in a finite-dimensional invariant de Rham model of the symmetric quotient of the Bogomolov--Guan precursor. Its indeterminacy vanishes, and its nontriviality is detected by an explicit top-degree pairing arising from an $S_{n+1}$-invariant tensor contraction. A nonzero-degree map then transfers nonformality to the smooth Bogomolov--Guan manifold. We also compare the Bogomolov--Guan precursor with the nilmanifold appearing in Guan's original construction, showing that they are related by a natural $S_{n+1}$-equivariant finite cover of degree $(n+1)^2$ and have the same invariant de Rham model. Using Guan's resolution, which we prove to be projective and semismall, together with Saito's decomposition theorem, we further show that the third Betti number of every Bogomolov--Guan manifold of complex dimension at least four is equal to $3$.

    We construct the integral Lawson--Deligne map of weight $q=n-p-k-1$ on smooth complex projective $n$-folds using filtered currents. A Picard--Fuchs separation argument applied to the conic and van Geemen normal functions on the mirror quintic determines explicit regulator subspaces modulo the full rational period group. For prescribed elliptic moduli and a suitable mirror-quintic fiber, the subspace generated by their $a$- and $b$-loop products has dimension twice the $\Q$-dimension of the period-monomial space. Moduli $i\sqrt{\ell_j}$ for distinct primes $\ell_j$ give $2^{k+1}$ independent images on varieties of dimension $p+k+2$; one repeated imaginary quadratic modulus gives dimension four for every $k\geq1$. Compatibility with known projective-bundle and blow-up decompositions yields independent exceptional subspaces on smooth rational varieties. We also compare the higher Chow composite with the Bloch--KLM regulator after lowering the Hodge filtration. The KLM representative reduces to a cut-current class, and equality with the Lawson composite is proved in degree zero and for constant-unit decomposable classes. The general positive-degree comparison is reduced to an explicit filtered-realization condition.

  46. [2608.22047] The trace field on the prey nullcline: criticality of planar Hopf bifurcations on the admissible branch
    The trace field on the prey nullcline: criticality of planar Hopf bifurcations on the admissible branch
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    Along the prey nullcline $y=g(x)$ of a planar system, the Jacobian entry satisfies $J_{11}=P\,g'$, where $P=-f_{1y}|_{\gamma}$. Under $P>0$ and predator self-damping, the Hopf trace condition forces $g'(x^\ast)>0$: critical points of $g$ are spectral barriers, so the Hopf locus lies on ascending branches. This geometric localization determines where oscillatory instability can occur; we determine how the bifurcation unfolds there. Let $D=f_{1x}+f_{2y}$ and $\tau=D|_{\gamma}$. Using nullcline coordinates $(u=x,v=y-g(x))$ and Hadamard's lemma gives $\dot u=-vH$, $\dot v=W$, with $H(u,0)=P$. The mixed jet of $W$ is determined by the jets of $\tau$ and $\nu=f_2|_{\gamma}$, yielding a closed five-term formula for the first Lyapunov coefficient $\ell_1$ in trace-field form, valid for predator-dependent functional responses. Three distinct reductions of the cubic jet are established. In general planar Hopf bifurcations, eigenvector pairing gives $\partial\ell_1/\partial f_{1yyy}=\partial\ell_1/\partial f_{2xxx}=0$. Straightening the nullcline removes two further slots, while within the Gause class the reparametrization $\varphi\mapsto g$ removes $p'''$. They have different scopes. For predator-dependent responses, comparison with the $y$-affine truncation of the $3$-jet at fixed linear part gives $\ell_1=\ell_1^{\mathrm{aff}}+\Delta$, where $\Delta=\Lambda\bigl(f_{1xyy}+\omega_0^{-2}\langle\partial_x f,\nabla D\rangle f_{1yy}\bigr)$ and $\Lambda>0$. On the Hopf locus, $\langle\partial_x f,\nabla D\rangle=J_{11}\tau'+(\omega_0^2/P)D_y$. Thus the same quantity $J_{11}=Pg'$ that localizes the bifurcation at first order reappears at third order as the weight of the tangential trace derivative. The bridge identity is independent of eigenvector normalization, while $\ell_1,\ell_1^{\mathrm{aff}},\Delta,\Lambda$ scale by $|c|^2$ under $q\mapsto cq$; their signs and ratios are unchanged.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22047

    Three Problems on Separable Quotients of Precompact Abelian Groups
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    We address three problems on separable quotients of topological groups posed by Leiderman, Morris, and Tkachenko in \cite{LMT} published on Israel Journal of Mathematics. These results answer Problem~1.25 negatively, Problem~3.12 affirmatively and realize all three regularity properties in Problem~3.14 simultaneously in \cite{LMT}.

  2. [2609.26201] Maximal Filters in the Lattice of Partitions of an Infinite Set
    Maximal Filters in the Lattice of Partitions of an Infinite Set
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    We study maximal (proper) filters in the complete lattice of partitions of an infinite set~$X$. In the language of uniform spaces, these are precisely the atoms of the lattice of zero-dimensional uniformities on $X$, introduced by Pelant and Reiterman and studied further by Pelant, Reiterman, R\"odl and Simon. The first half of this paper recovers, sharpens, and extends their classification in purely partition-theoretic terms. {Call a maximal filter of partitions {\em type I} if it does not contain all finite partitions, and {\em type II} if it does.} Type I filters are induced, in an essentially unique way, by ultrafilters on families of pairwise disjoint doubletons. {A type II filter} determines a non-principal ultrafilter on $X$, the \emph{heart}; the heart determines the filter precisely when {the former} is minimal in the Rudin--Keisler order. Each member $F$ of {a type II filter} gives rise to a closed \emph{fiber} in $X^{*} = \beta X \setminus X$, {consisting of} the set of ultrafilters agreeing with the heart on $F$. We prove a trichotomy describing the topology of arbitrary fibers. We then show that the fibers do not encode the filter: fibers do not form a semilattice under intersection, the closure of an infinite discrete set of ultrafilters of a single Rudin--Keisler type (a \emph{sparse} set, in our terminology) need not be a fiber, and a partition incompatible with a member of the filter may have a {fiber strictly larger than that member.} A representation that does succeed is nevertheless available in another category: the {type-II filters} with heart $\mathfrak{u}$ correspond to the maximal proper substructures of the ultrapower $X^X\!/\mathfrak{u}$ of the full structure on $X$. The topological representation problem remains open.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26201

    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究视觉边界的一种对数重规范，它源于 Garg-Jana-Qing 引入的次线性 Morse 边界上的对数度量。对于每个真测地双曲空间 X，我们证明 d_log(ξ,η) ≍ (1+(ξ|η)_o)^{-θ}。作为推论，在边界的自然等同下，d_log 与径向密度 g_θ(t)=(1+t)^{-1-θ} 关联的多项式 Floyd 边界度量双 Lipschitz 等价。真测地双曲空间之间的每个拟等距诱导其对数边界之间具有固定容许指数的双 Lipschitz 映射。对于每个非初等双曲群，对数边界是非加倍且具有无限 Hausdorff 和 Assouad 维数。然而，对数重规范保持 Nagata（容量）维数，并继续恢复群的渐近维数。它还通过线性连通性（有界转折）检测单端性，并保持一致完美性。因此，对数度量与视觉度量不是拟对称等价的，尽管若干粗几何特征在对数边界中仍然可见。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study a logarithmic re-gauging of the visual boundary, arising from the logarithmic metric on the sublinear Morse boundary introduced by Garg-Jana-Qing. For every proper geodesic hyperbolic space $X$, we prove that $d_{\mathrm{log}}(\xi,\eta)\asymp (1+(\xi\mid\eta)_o)^{-\theta}$. As a consequence, under the natural identification of boundaries, $d_{\mathrm{log}}$ is bi-Lipschitz equivalent to the polynomial Floyd boundary metric associated with the radial density $g_\theta(t)=(1+t)^{-1-\theta}$. Every quasi-isometry between proper geodesic hyperbolic spaces induces a bi-Lipschitz map between their logarithmic boundaries with a fixed admissible exponent. For every non-elementary hyperbolic group, the logarithmic boundary is nondoubling and has infinite Hausdorff and Assouad dimensions. Nevertheless, the logarithmic re-gauging preserves Nagata (capacity) dimension and continues to recover the asymptotic dimension of the group. It also detects one-endedness through linear connectedness (bounded turning) and preserves uniform perfectness. Thus the logarithmic and visual metrics are not quasisymmetrically equivalent, although several coarse-geometric features remain visible in the logarithmic boundary.

    📖 中文摘要

    设 Σ 是紧致有向曲面。我们研究由 M. Goussarov 和 K. Habiro 引入的 Σ 上同调柱的分次李代数。为分析其挠，我们改进了由 Y. Nozaki、M. Suzuki 和第三作者提出的策略，该策略依赖于 LMO 函子的模 1 约化和 clasp 计算。具体地，我们发展了在辛群标准作用下研究该李代数奇数阶分量中挠的 Sp-模结构的一般工具。我们证明该挠部分满射到一个 2-挠 Sp-模，该模用 Jacobi 图显式描述。作为应用，我们给出同调柱李代数三次分量所给出的 Sp-模的内在描述。进一步的动机是理解 Σ 的 Torelli 群下中心列关联分次中的挠现象：在此方向上，我们显式展示了一个 Sp-模，Torelli 李代数的挠在三次上满射到该模。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\Sigma$ be a compact oriented surface. We investigate the graded Lie algebra of homology cylinders over $\Sigma$, as introduced by M. Goussarov and K. Habiro. To analyze its torsion, we refine a strategy initiated by Y. Nozaki, M. Suzuki, and the third author, which relies on the reduction modulo $1$ of the LMO functor and on clasper calculus. Specifically, we develop general tools for studying, under the standard action of the symplectic group, the Sp-module structure of the torsion in the odd-degree component of this Lie algebra. We show that this torsion part surjects onto an $2$-torsion Sp-module, which is explicitly described in terms of Jacobi diagrams. As an application, we provide an intrinsic description of the Sp-module given by the degree-three component of the Lie algebra of homology cylinders. A further motivation is to understand torsion phenomena in the associated graded of the lower central series of the Torelli group of $\Sigma$: in this direction, we exhibit an explicit Sp-module onto which the torsion of the Torelli Lie algebra surjects in degree three.

    🤖 AI 总结

    • 推广到极小几何表示，定义共轭类正规形式。
    • 提供求根和共轭问题的高效算法及增长率应用。
    💡 研究曲面群对称表示，给出幂的正规形式、字长公式及共轭和求根算法。
    📖 中文摘要

    在本文中，我们主要研究曲面群的以下对称表示：π_1(Σ_g)=⟨c_1,…,c_{2g} | c_1…c_{2g}c_1^{-1}…c_{2g}^{-1}⟩。对于每个非平凡元素x∈π_1(Σ_g)和k≥2，我们得到x^k在长度字典序下正规形式的一致代表：nf(x^k)=LW^{k-2}R。基于此结果，我们建立这些正规形式之间的新关系，并推导出以下三个与字长相关的公式：|x^2|>|x|；|x^k|=(k-1)(|x^2|-|x|)+|x|；lim_{k→∞}|x^k|/k=|x^2|-|x|。此外，我们将这些结果推广到每个极小几何表示，得到更粗糙的类似物。然后我们定义π_1(Σ_g)中共轭类的正规形式，并提供判断群元素共轭的准则。作为推论，我们提供求解求根和共轭问题的高效算法。最后，我们给出在计算若干增长率中的应用。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we mainly study the following symmetric presentation of the surface group $$\pi_1(\Sigma_g)=\left\langle c_1,\dots, c_{2g}\mid c_1\cdots c_{2g}c_1^{-1}\cdots c_{2g}^{-1}\right\rangle.$$ For every nontrivial element $x\in \pi_1(\Sigma_g)$ and $k\geq 2$, we obtain a uniform representative of the normal forms $\mathfrak{nf}(x^k)$ of $x^k$ under the length-lexicographical order: $$\mathfrak{nf}(x^k) = \overline{LW^{k-2}R}.$$ Building on this result, we establish a new relation among these normal forms, and then derive the following three formulae related to the word length: $|x^2|>|x|$; $|x^k|=(k-1)(|x^2|-|x|)+|x|$; $\lim_{k\to\infty}\frac{|x^k|}{k}=|x^2|-|x|$. Furthermore, we extend these results to obtain a coarser analogue for every minimal geometric presentation. We then define normal forms of conjugacy classes in $\pi_1(\Sigma_g)$ and provide a criterion for determining the conjugacy of group elements. As a consequence, we provide efficient algorithms for solving the root-finding and conjugacy problems. Finally, we present applications to the computation of several growth rates.

  1. [2609.25151] The universal valued Abelian groups of Niemiec are L\'{e}vy, strongly exotic, extremely amenable, and $\mathbb{G}_r(0)$ is generically monothetic
    The universal valued Abelian groups of Niemiec are L\'{e}vy, strongly exotic, extremely amenable, and $\mathbb{G}_r(0)$ is generically monothetic
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove various properties of the universal valued Abelian groups $\mathbb{G}_r(N)$ constructed by Niemiec, for $r\in\{1,\infty\}$ and $N\in\{0,2,3,\ldots\}$. First we show that the completion of $\Gamma_0=\bigoplus_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbb{Q}/\mathbb{Z}$ (for $N=0$) or $\Gamma_N=\bigoplus_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbb{Z}/N\mathbb{Z}$ (for $N\geq 2$) with respect to a generic invariant metric, bounded by $1$ when $r=1$, is isometrically group-isomorphic to $\mathbb{G}_r(N)$, recovering a result of Doucha in the case when $r=\infty $ and $N=0$. This confirms an expectation of Doucha for the group $\Gamma_N$. We combine this genericity result with a criterion of Melleray and Tsankov concerning the extreme amenability of the generic completion of a countable group to obtain the extreme amenability of $\mathbb{G}_r(N)$. We then establish the strictly stronger properties that these groups are L\'{e}vy and strongly exotic. We conclude by showing that $\mathbb{G}_r(0)$ is also monothetic, from which we obtain the existence of a monothetic group structure on the Urysohn sphere, giving a bounded version of a result by Cameron and Vershik and answering a question of Niemiec.

    设 G 是无挠群，其实群代数 R[G] 无零因子，设 u ↦ u^{*θ} 是 R[G] 的 l2-等距反对合（由反演对合与环自同构和符号特征的复合）。我们证明一个紧致性定理：对于每个有限 *θ-闭支撑窗口 W，常数 μ*(W) = min{ ||w^{*θ}w||_2 : ||w||_2 = 1, supp(w) ⊆ W } 严格为正，且每个支撑在 W 中的实 θ-酉元素（u^{*θ}u = 1）满足 ||u||_2 ≤ μ*(W)^{-1/2}。特别地，支撑在 W 中的整数 θ-酉元素形成一个有限、可有效枚举的集合：单位搜索的先验无限“高度”方向坍缩为单个实常数。对于 Promislow（Hantzsche-Wendt）群 P 和承载 Gardam 对单位猜想在 F_2 上反例的窗口 B(4)，我们将此与精确 SAT 认证的高度阶梯（每层高度 ≤ 31，更大高度处有 DRAT 认证的主单元）、无半径深度尾定理以及 ± 层之间的镜像对称相结合，将所有支撑在 B(4) 中的非平凡 θ-酉单位 u = ±1 (mod 2) 的消失归约到 μ*(B(4)) 的单个认证下界。我们报告数值估计 μ*(B(4)) ~ 1.4e-3，远高于所需阈值，并证明关于剩余认证问题的三个结构结果：不存在线性（Cauchy-Schwarz）对偶证书，因为 P 携带 θ-反酉平凡元素；且数值上，水平 2 平方和松弛是边界固定的，具有显式斜率，在直接和理想乘子表述中均如此——障碍是一个虚假伪矩，没有测度能实现它。我们还将该机制与 Z[D_∞] 进行对比，其中挠产生零因子，μ* = 0，且存在真正无界的扭曲酉元素幂零族。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let G be a torsion-free group whose real group algebra R[G] has no zero divisors, and let u -> u^{*theta} be an l2-isometric anti-involution of R[G] (a composition of the inversion involution with a ring automorphism and a sign character). We prove a compactness theorem: for every finite *theta-closed support window W the constant mu*(W) = min{ ||w^{*theta}w||_2 : ||w||_2 = 1, supp(w) in W } is strictly positive, and every real theta-unitary element (u^{*theta}u = 1) supported in W satisfies ||u||_2 <= mu*(W)^{-1/2}. In particular the integer theta-unitary elements supported in W form a finite, effectively enumerable set: the a priori infinite "height" direction of the unit search collapses to a single real constant. For the Promislow (Hantzsche-Wendt) group P and the window B(4) that hosts Gardam's counterexample to the unit conjecture over F_2, we combine this with exact SAT-certified height ladders (heights <= 31 per stratum, DRAT-certified master cell at larger heights), a radius-free depth-tail theorem, and a mirror symmetry between the +- strata, reducing the vanishing of all nontrivial theta-unitary units u = +-1 (mod 2) with supp(u) in B(4) to a single certified lower bound on mu*(B(4)). We report numerical estimates mu*(B(4)) ~ 1.4e-3, well above the required threshold, and prove three structural results about the remaining certification problem: no linear (Cauchy-Schwarz) dual certificate exists, because P carries theta-anti-unitary trivial elements; and, numerically, the level-2 sum-of-squares relaxation is boundary-pinned with an explicit slope, both in the direct and in the ideal-multiplier formulation -- the obstruction being a spurious pseudo-moment that no measure can realize. We also contrast the mechanism with Z[D_infinity], where torsion produces zero divisors, mu* = 0, and genuinely unbounded unipotent families of twisted unitaries.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25015
  5. [2609.25016] Nielsen-Soelberg 群环的整环问题：交换情形及认证球检查
    The domain question for the Nielsen-Soelberg group rings: the commutative case, with certified ball checks
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 Nielsen-Soelberg 群环的整环问题。
    • 证明对于交换整环系数，三个群环都是整环。
    • 问题对非交换系数整环仍然开放。
    • 提供机器可验证的球内无零因子认证。
    💡 证明对于交换整环系数，Nielsen-Soelberg 的三个群环都是整环，并给出机器可验证的球内无零因子认证。
    📖 中文摘要

    Nielsen 和 Soelberg 展示了三个无挠群 G_1, G_2, G_3 携带没有唯一乘积的 8 元素集，并询问是否有群环 R[G_i]（R 为整环）是整环。我们记录：对于每个交换整环 R，三个群的答案都是肯定的：每个 G_i 是几乎幂零的，因此 Kropholler-Linnell-Moody 定理适用于每个域、每个特征，且交换系数可约化到分式域。除 G_2 的相关有限指数子群的幂零结构外，每个要素都在文献中，该结构由我们 companion 论文（arXiv:2607.19687）的认证计算模型提供。因此问题恰好对非交换系数整环仍然开放，其中唯一乘积机制——唯一已知的环无关机制——正是这些群被构造来缺乏的。作为补充，我们报告机器可检查、DRAT 认证的验证，即 F_2[G_i] 在定义生成集的显式球中没有零因子，仅通过命题推理获得，独立于 K-理论机制；我们精确说明这些证书添加和不添加什么。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Nielsen and Soelberg exhibited three torsion-free groups G_1, G_2, G_3 carrying 8-element sets without unique products, and asked whether any of the group rings R[G_i], R a domain, is a domain. We record that for every commutative domain R the answer is affirmative for all three groups: each G_i is virtually nilpotent, so the theorem of Kropholler, Linnell and Moody applies over every field, in every characteristic, and commutative coefficients reduce to the fraction field. Every ingredient is in the literature except the nilpotent structure of the relevant finite-index subgroup of G_2, which is supplied by the certified computational model of our companion paper (arXiv:2607.19687). The question therefore remains open exactly for noncommutative coefficient domains, where the unique-product mechanism -- the only known ring-independent one -- is precisely what these groups are constructed to lack. As a complement we report machine-checkable, DRAT-certified verifications that F_2[G_i] has no zero divisors with both supports in explicit balls of the defining generating sets, obtained by propositional reasoning alone, independent of the K-theoretic machinery; we state precisely what these certificates do and do not add.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25016

We prove a sufficient condition for the measure of a verbal set to be zero in a free profinite group of finite rank, from which we deduce that certain existentially definable sets in free profinite groups of finite rank can only have measure zero or one. Finally, we compute the measure of definable sets in torsion-free finitely generated abelian groups.

A finite basis theorem for finite semirings with a multiplicative identity establishes finite basedness of all finite truncations. Finally, we determine the finite basis properties of two families of subvarieties defined by power identities and of subvarieties relatively defined by arbitrary families of identities making individual additive subterms greatest elements.

    Nonfinitely based intervals of power semiring varieties
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究有限群幂半环生成的加法幂等半环簇的有限基性质。
    • 证明在特定区间内所有簇均非有限基，且无固定变量数上界。
    • 构造等式上界并得到更多非有限基区间。

    💡 证明有限群幂半环生成的簇区间中所有簇均非有限基，且无固定变量数上界。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究加法幂等半环簇格中与有限群的幂半环相关的区间。我们证明，每个位于阶至少为三的有限群的非空幂半环之上、且位于由有限多个有限群的全幂半环生成的簇之下的簇都是非有限基的。事实上，这些簇中没有一个允许具有固定有限变量数上界的等式基。特别地，添加空集得到一个完全由非有限基簇组成的区间。我们还构造了在零添加下封闭的等式上界，并产生具有相同性质的进一步区间。证明结合了有限交换商半环、有限模上核阻断子的基数估计以及有限群中有序乘积纤维的一致估计。作为推论，有限群的全幂半环是有限基的当且仅当该群至多有两个元素。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study intervals in the lattice of additively idempotent semiring varieties associated with power semirings of finite groups. In fact, none of these varieties admits an identity basis with a fixed finite bound on the number of variables. In particular, adjoining the empty set gives an interval consisting entirely of nonfinitely based varieties. We also construct equational upper bounds that are closed under zero adjunction and yield further intervals with the same property. The proof combines finite commutative quotient semirings, a cardinality estimate for kernel blockers over finite modules, and a uniform estimate for fibres of ordered products in finite groups. As a consequence, the full power semiring of a finite group is finitely based precisely when the group has at most two elements.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们给出AF-by-离散群胚的指数映射的显式几何和代数描述。指数记录无限瓦片之间的传输和芽群中的阿贝尔化返回，受相关Bratteli图维数群中缺陷的约束。我们确定指数映射的核，并构造在单点轨道处具有平凡芽的零指数元素的转置分解。特别地，在每个极小情形中，该核等于由动力转置生成的子群。我们还刻画了由固定AF核心的逼近，并证明紧生成的极小系统的几乎有限性。我们用芽群显式表达高次同调。我们通过提升的曲面关系和由平衡边界数据构造的扩张类来表达次级奇偶微分。在极小性和比较性下，提升有限关系给出分裂的AH序列。显式的边界和矩阵计算，以及一个具有非零奇偶微分的非极小例子，说明了该理论。

Under minimality and comparison, lifting finite relations gives a split AH sequence.   13. [2609.26228] 有限可解群不必具有递增不可细化子群链
    Finite Soluble Groups Need Not Admit Increasing Unrefinable Subgroup Chains
    🤖 AI 总结

    • 构造无限族可解矩阵群作为反例，否定Monakhov-Sokhor问题。
    • 最小反例阶为419904，基于Kohler的例子。
    • 障碍由三个极大子群指数的计算得出。
    💡 构造反例否定Monakhov-Sokhor问题，证明存在无递增不可细化子群链的有限可解群。
    📖 中文摘要

    在2026年8月8日开始的通信中，V. S. Monakhov和I. L. Sokhor向作者提出以下问题：是否每个有限群都有一条子群链，其连续指数非递减且不可细化？我们对此问题给出否定回答，即使在可解群中也是如此。对于每个奇素数p，我们构造一个在p元域上次数为5的可解矩阵群。该群的阶为64乘以p的八次方，且没有这样的链。这些反例构成一个无限族，其最小成员（当p等于3时）的阶为419904。构造基于Kohler的一个例子，障碍来自三个极大子群指数的初等计算。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In correspondence beginning on August 8, 2026, V. S. Monakhov and I. L. Sokhor communicated the following question to the author: does every finite group have an unrefinable chain of subgroups whose successive indices are nondecreasing? We answer this question in the negative, even among soluble groups. For every odd prime p, we construct a soluble matrix group of degree five over the field with p elements. The group has order 64 times the eighth power of p and has no such chain. These counterexamples form an infinite family whose smallest member, obtained when p equals three, has order 419904. The construction is based on an example due to Kohler, and the obstruction follows from three elementary calculations of maximal subgroup indices.

  14. [2609.26307] 置换群中的字长与直径
    Word Length and Diameter in Permutation Groups
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究置换群中二元直径问题和二元长度问题的计算复杂性。
    • 证明二元直径问题是PSPACE完全的。
    • 在2步幂零群中，直径问题为Π₂^P完全，长度问题为NP完全。
    • 无限制时长度问题为PSPACE完全。
    💡 证明二元直径问题是PSPACE完全的，在2步幂零群中直径问题为Π₂^P完全，长度问题为NP完全。
    📖 中文摘要

    二元直径问题的输入由显式表示的生成有限群G的置换和一个二元编码的非负整数k组成。问题是G的每个元素是否都是至多k个输入生成元的乘积。对于二元长度问题，输入还包含一个置换g∈G，并询问g是否是至多k个输入生成元的乘积。我们证明二元直径问题是PSPACE完全的。当限制在2步幂零群时，二元直径问题被证明是Π₂^P完全的，而二元长度问题被证明是NP完全的。在没有2步幂零群限制的情况下，二元长度问题由Jerrum的结果是PSPACE完全的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The input for the binary diameter problem consists of explicitly represented permutations generating a finite group $G$ and a binary-encoded nonnegative integer $k$. The question is whether every element of $G$ is a product of at most $k$ input generators. For the binary length problem, the input contains in addition a permutation $g \in G$ and it is asked whether $g$ is a product of at most $k$ input generators. We prove that the binary diameter problem is PSPACE-complete. When restricted to $2$-step nilpotent groups, the binary diameter problem is shown to be complete for $\mathsf{\Pi_2^P}$, whereas the binary length problem is shown to be NP-complete. Without the restriction to $2$-step nilpotent groups, the binary length problem is PSPACE-complete by a result of Jerrum.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26307

    • 建立幂族与线性族的包含准则，确定并的有限基性质。
    • 实现六元极限半环为八元线性字半环的商，解决极小性问题。
    💡 研究由整除序定义的半环的有限基性质，证明线性字半环有限基当且仅当长度≤2，并确定并的有限基。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究由交换字通过赋予其子字整除序得到的加法幂等半环。每个与单字母幂相关的有限半环是有限基的，而与线性字相关的有限半环是有限基的当且仅当该字长度至多2。一个超图保持引理给出非有限基结果并将其推广到簇的区间。我们建立幂族和线性族之间的尖锐包含准则，并确定由每个族的一个成员生成的两个簇的每个并的有限基性质。无限制幂族生成非有限基的max-plus簇。相反，无限制线性族具有有限基，其与每个有限幂成员的并也是如此。我们还实现了一个已知的六元极限半环作为八元线性字半环的子半环的商。这给出了一个真非有限基子簇并解决了相应的极小性问题。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study additively idempotent semirings obtained from commutative words by equipping their subwords with the divisibility order. Every finite semiring associated with a power of one letter is finitely based, whereas one associated with a linear word is finitely based exactly when the word has length at most two. A hypergraph preservation lemma yields the nonfinite basis result and extends it to intervals of varieties. We establish a sharp containment criterion between the power and linear families, and determine the finite basis property of every join of two varieties generated by one member of each family. The unrestricted power family generates the nonfinitely based max-plus variety. In contrast, the unrestricted linear family has a finite basis, as does its join with each finite power member. We also realize a previously known six-element limit semiring as a quotient of a subsemiring of the eight-element linear-word semiring. This gives a proper nonfinitely based subvariety and resolves the corresponding minimality question.

    Refining invariants of finite groups with class functions

    • 刻画类函数与子群过渡的相容性，加强Sangroniz定理。
    • 证明实元素类函数总是特征标，并给出阿贝尔Sylow p-子群准则。
    💡 研究有限群类函数作为特征标的条件，证明两类重要情形下为特征标，并刻画子群相容性。
    📖 中文摘要

    有限群的许多数值不变量作为自然关联的类函数中平凡特征标的重数出现，其余重数携带更精细的结构信息。对于阶仅涉及选定素数集的共轭类数目，自然的类函数是介于经典共轭特征标和正则特征标之间的广义特征标。我们证明它在两个重要的一般情形中是特征标，这两个情形由Robinson独立获得，并且我们排除了一族群和特征标，我们认为反例最可能出现在其中。此外，我们刻画这些类函数何时与过渡到子群相容，从而加强了Sangroniz的一个定理。对于实元素的类似类函数被证明总是给出特征标。最后，我们给出一个对偶构造，结合最近证明的McKay猜想，产生有限群具有阿贝尔Sylow p-子群的最佳可能准则。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Many numerical invariants of a finite group arise as the multiplicity of the trivial character in a naturally associated class function, and the remaining multiplicities carry finer structural information. For the number of conjugacy classes of elements whose order involves only a chosen set of primes, the natural such class function is a generalized character interpolating between the classical conjugating character and the regular character. We prove that it is a character in two important general cases, obtained independently by Robinson, and we rule out a family of groups and characters in which, we argue, a counterexample would be most likely to arise. Furthermore, we characterize when these class functions are compatible with passing to a subgroup and thereby sharpen a theorem of Sangroniz. The analogous class function for real elements is shown to always give a character. Finally, we give a dual construction that, together with the recently proved McKay Conjecture, yields a best-possible criterion for a finite group to have an abelian Sylow $p$-subgroup.

    🌐 English Abstract

    We reconstruct the generalized hexagon of order $(2,8)$ from an abstract even rank-$26$ lattice of determinant $3$ and minimum $4$, and conversely reconstruct the lattice from the hexagon. Harmonic theta identities determine the $819$ shortest vectors in a non-zero discriminant class and their association scheme, and show that every non-empty positive-norm shell of the lattice and its dual is a spherical $5$-design. In the converse direction, the rank-$26$ idempotent gives the lattice, with saturation proved by a short dual-coset argument. A positive-definite Niemeier construction proves existence and uniqueness of the lattice and hence of the hexagon. The third moment of a projective $3$-design in the Cayley plane reconstructs the traceless Albert product. This upgrades angle-preserving bijections to elements of $F_4(\mathbb R)$ and proves geometric uniqueness of the tight $819$-point projective $5$-design. This proves uniqueness and gives the automorphism-group order. The group is identified afterward as ${}^3D_4(2):3$, with a central factor $C_2$ for the full lattice group. The reconstruction and deletion arguments parallel the length-$26$ binary code construction.

    📖 中文摘要

    🌐 English Abstract

    A band is a semigroup in which each element is idempotent. In recent years, there has been a lot of activity on the representation theory of the subclass of left regular bands due to connections to Markov chains associated to hyperplane arrangements, oriented matroids, matroids and CAT(0) cube complexes. We prove here that the integral semigroup algebra of a band is isomorphic to the integral path algebra of a quiver modulo an admissible ideal. This leads to a uniform bound quiver presentation for band algebras over all fields. Also, we answer a question of Margolis, Saliola and Steinberg by proving that the integral semigroup algebra of a CW left regular band is isomorphic to the quotient of the integral path algebra of the Hasse diagram of its support semilattice modulo the ideal generated by the sum of all paths of length two. We finally show that, for lattices in connected semisimple real Lie groups with finite center and no compact factors, the scalar-expectation case of the corresponding classification is equivalent to ordinary ITAP.

  2. [2410.20071] $\beta$-Uniform Convexity and Divisible Domains
    $\beta$-Uniform Convexity and Divisible Domains
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    Divisible convex sets have long been important in the study of Hilbert geometries. When a divisible convex set is an ellipsoid, the Hilbert geometry it induces is the hyperbolic space. In general, strictly convex divisible domains exhibit negative curvature properties, but only the ellipsoid is a CAT(0) space. The notion of p-uniform convexity from the theory of Banach spaces has been proposed by Shin-Ichi Ohta as a generalization of the Alexandrov-Toponogov comparison theorems to Finsler manifolds. We prove that a natural Finsler metric on a strictly convex divisible domain is $\beta$-uniformly convex, where the constant $\beta$ is related to the regularity of the boundary. We use this to show, with AI assistance, that the Hilbert metric, under suitable local and scale-dependent assumptions, is $\beta$-uniformly convex on such domains.

  3. [2609.26560] Continuity of Magnitude at Finite Subsets of $\ell_1^N$

    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

In this paper, we prove that magnitude is continuous at every finite subset $F$ of $\ell_1^N$. We do this by first deriving the weight measure for a finite union of cubes, i.e.\ cubical thickenings of the points in $F$, and then showing that these thickenings converge to the magnitude of the underlying finite set.

  4. [2609.25035] Heilbronn三角形问题中的KKT应力、仿射矩与分离子通量
    KKT Stresses, Affine Moments, and Separator Flux in the Heilbronn Triangle Problem
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Heilbronn三角形问题的变分应力理论。
    • 在局部最优处，KKT乘子构成斜对称矩阵并满足平衡方程。
    • 导出各向同性仿射矩恒等式和应力分量性质。
    • 引入混合算子处理非唯一乘子并排除某些残差。
    • 结果隔离了退化性但未完全解决问题。
    💡 发展Heilbronn三角形问题的变分应力理论，导出KKT乘子结构和仿射矩恒等式。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于单位正方形中的n个点，Heilbronn三角形问题要求最大可能的最小三角形面积。我们为这个最大最小问题发展变分应力理论。在每个正面积局部最优处，归一化的Karush-Kuhn-Tucker乘子组成一个斜对称矩阵B，满足Bz=2ib，其中b是向外的正方形反作用力。该平衡具有各向同性仿射矩sum_i p_i b_i^T = Delta I_2；该恒等式对每个紧子族也成立，权重继承自同一乘子，Delta按其乘子质量缩放。由此推出每个正应力分量与所有四条边相交，且至多有两个这样的分量。为处理非唯一乘子，我们引入整个KKT面上应力核的交集和包含所有紧行列式与边界导数的规范混合算子。严格凸选择子消除所有可分解不可见运动，留下无秩一残差并具有尖锐维数界；字面最大二维残差被排除。两端子应力满足精确界面通量律，一个五内部顶点秩四块具有Pfaffian余因子载体。最后，解析约化后精确符号枚举排除了指定单静默五循环残差的所有单外部完成，对所有方向词。这些结果隔离了剩余退化性，但未解决任意n的问题。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For n points in the unit square, the Heilbronn triangle problem asks for the largest possible minimum triangle area. We develop a variational stress theory for this max-min problem. At every positive-area local optimum, normalized Karush-Kuhn-Tucker multipliers assemble into a skew matrix B satisfying Bz = 2ib, where b is the outward square reaction. This equilibrium has the isotropic affine moment sum_i p_i b_i^T = Delta I_2; the identity also holds for every tight subfamily carrying weights inherited from the same multiplier, with Delta scaled by its multiplier mass. It follows that every positive stress component meets all four sides and that there are at most two such components. To handle nonunique multipliers, we introduce the intersection of the stress kernels over the whole KKT face and a canonical hybrid operator incorporating all tight determinant and boundary derivatives. A strictly convex selector removes every decomposable invisible motion, leaving a rank-one-free residual with a sharp dimension bound; the literal maximal two-dimensional residual is excluded. Two-terminal substresses satisfy an exact interface-flux law, and a five-internal-vertex rank-four block has a Pfaffian cofactor carrier. Finally, an analytic reduction followed by exact symbolic enumeration excludes every one-external completion of a specified one-silent five-cycle residual, for all orientation words. These results isolate the remaining degeneracy but do not solve the problem for arbitrary n.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25035
  5. [2609.25288] Rado覆盖问题的指数改进
    Exponential improvements in Rado's covering problem
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Rado覆盖问题中单位球的不相交子集合体积比例下界。
    • 利用构造性方法首次指数改进下界。
    • 将估计差距缩小到2.910^{-d}与2.447^{-d}之间。
    • 方法给出多项式时间算法。
    • 并推广到满足一致凸性的对称凸体。
    💡 首次指数改进Rado覆盖问题下界，将估计差距缩小到2.910^{-d}与2.447^{-d}之间。
    📖 中文摘要

    设B^d表示d维欧几里得单位球。最大的常数f(B^d)∈[0,1]是什么，使得每个有限单位球集合C在R^d中都有一个不相交子集合S，其体积至少占C体积的f(B^d)？这个问题由T. Rado于1928年首次提出，针对平面中轴平行正方形；作者受Vitali经典覆盖引理启发。欧几里得球的情形由R. Rado于1949年首次考虑。直到去年，单位球f(B^d)的最佳已知估计相差甚远：(1+ε_d)3^{-d} ≤ f(B^d) ≤ 2^{-d}，其中0<ε_d=o_{d→∞}(1)。最近，本文作者观察到上界的指数改进可由Kabatiansky-Levenshtein球码界推出，而下界由C. Xie和G. Ge改进了线性因子（见arxiv:2608.09744）。当前大d的最佳估计为：c·d·3^{-d} ≤ f(B^d) ≤ 2.447^{-d}，其中c>0是绝对常数。这里我们给出近80年来下界的首次指数改进，将差距缩小到：2.910^{-d} ≤ f(B^d) ≤ 2.447^{-d}。我们的方法是构造性的，并给出多项式时间算法来找到实现该估计的不相交子集合。此外，相同技术对所有满足一致凸性假设的对称凸体给出类似的指数改进下界，例如所有p∈(1,∞)的ℓ^p球。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $B^d$ denote the $d$-dimensional Euclidean ball of unit radius. What is the largest constant $f(B^d) \in [0,1]$ with the property that every finite collection $\mathcal{C}$ of unit balls in $\mathbb{R}^d$ admits a disjoint sub-collection $\mathcal{S}$ occupying at least a fraction $f(B^d)$ of the volume of $\mathcal{C}$? This problem was first raised by T. Rad\'o in 1928, for axis-parallel squares in the plane; the author was motivated by a classical covering lemma in real analysis due to Vitali. The case of Euclidean balls was first considered by R. Rado in 1949. Until last year the best known estimates on $f(B^d)$ for unit balls where very far apart: \[ (1+\epsilon_d) 3^{-d} \leq f(B^d) \leq 2^{-d}, \] where $0<\epsilon_d=o_{d\rightarrow \infty}(1)$. Recently, the authors of this note observed that an exponential improvement on the upper bound follows from the Kabatiansky--Levenshtein spherical code bound, while the lower bound was improved by a linear factor by C.~Xie and G.~Ge (see arxiv:2608.09744). The current best estimates for large $d$ are \[ c \cdot d \cdot 3^{-d} \leq f(B^d) \leq 2.447^{-d}, \] where $c>0$ is an absolute constant. Here we offer the first exponential improvement of the lower bound in almost 80 years, which narrows the gap to: \[ 2.910^{-d} \leq f(B^d) \leq 2.447^{-d}. \] Our method is constructive and yields a polynomial time algorithm for finding a disjoint sub-collection realizing the estimate. Moreover the same technique gives similar exponentially improved lower bounds for all symmetric convex bodies satisfying a uniform convexity assumption, e.g., $\ell^p$-balls for all $p\in (1,\infty)$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25288

    🌐 English Abstract

    Nearest-neighbor graphs are discrete objects whose membership may change under small perturbations of the underlying coordinates. We establish an explicit stability guarantee for finite labeled configurations in an arbitrary metric space. If the gap between the kth and (k+1)st distances is positive, simultaneous per-label perturbations smaller than one quarter of that gap preserve the directed k-nearest-neighbor membership. The factor four is sharp under the stated uniform displacement assumptions, and every fixed deterministic construction based solely on the labeled neighbor family is consequently invariant. Under an interval-valid Lipschitz bound for labeled information-space trajectories, the same result yields a certified lower bound on the first possible rewiring time, with a refinement for label-specific motion bounds. We apply the finite theorem to frozen standardized Taylor-Green future-information coordinates [d_B, log A_B] for 585 particles at three observed time strata. Outward-rounded interval arithmetic certified a sufficient perturbation radius of approximately 1.023 x 10^-6, and a separately implemented checker within the same research workflow verified the rank and distance-margin calculations. An outcome-blind audit then examined whether the construction supported a numerical continuous-time horizon. Because the complete information map involved discrete clustering and boundary reconstruction and lacked an analytic derivative bound, validated dense-time bound, or certified modulus of continuity, the application was correctly classified as DISCRETE_ONLY with STOP_NO_INTERVAL_VALID_BOUND. Thus finite local structural invariance is proved and numerically certified for the frozen configuration, while temporal specialization and predictive generalization remain separate questions requiring additional evidence.

  7. [2609.26129] 定性凸性与通用横截面
    Qualitative convexity and universal cross-sections
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究凸体横截面路径的渐近行为。
    • 引入尾不变量并证明其总是连续统。
    • 证明横截面路径局部可整流但精确实现有障碍。
    • 而尾可实现任意连续统。
    • 构造具有几乎通用点的凸体，结果在Lean 4中形式化。
    💡 研究凸体横截面路径的渐近行为，引入尾不变量并证明其可实现性，结果在Lean 4中形式化。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究凸性中一类“大小无关”的问题：仅记录凸横截面到平移和缩放，因此附加到凸体B和方向ρ的数据是紧度量空间A_{n-1}中对齐形状的一条路径。感兴趣的对象是当切割超平面接近最后支撑超平面时该路径的渐近行为，由我们称为尾的不变量T(B,ρ)编码。我们证明尾总是连续统，多面体和光滑支撑点是“无聊的”（尾是一个点），而非无聊行为迫使退化接触。我们证明横截面路径局部可整流，每个局部可整流路径可近似实现，且稠密类可精确实现，但精确实现一般失败：有界转弯型的二阶障碍产生一条可整流路径但不是横截面路径。相比之下，对于尾，没有这种限制：每个形状连续统都精确作为尾出现，而非近似。我们构造具有几乎通用点的体，在这些点处重整化横截面任意好地逼近每个平面（更一般地(n-1)维）凸形状；这样的点可以在边界中稠密，并在嫁接位点具有任意指定的尾。以下所有结果已在Lean 4中形式验证。我们以几个优化问题和高余维变体结束。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study a family of questions in convexity in which \emph{size does not matter}: one records a convex cross-section only up to translation and scaling, so that the data attached to a convex body $B$ and a direction $\rho$ is a path in the compact metric space $\A_{n-1}$ of \emph{aligned shapes}. The object of interest is the asymptotic behaviour of this path as the cutting hyperplane approaches the last supporting hyperplane, encoded by an invariant $T(B,\rho)$ that we call the \emph{tail}. We show that tails are always continua, that polyhedral and smooth support points are ``boring'' (the tail is a point), and that non-boring behaviour forces degenerate contact. We show that cross-section paths are locally rectifiable, that every locally rectifiable path is realisable approximately and a dense class exactly, and that exact realisation fails in general: a second-order obstruction of bounded-turning type produces a rectifiable path that is not a cross-section path. For tails, by contrast, no such restriction survives: every continuum of shapes occurs as a tail, on the nose rather than up to approximation. We construct bodies possessing \emph{nearly universal} points, at which the renormalised cross-sections approximate every planar (more generally $(n-1)$-dimensional) convex shape arbitrarily well; such points can be made dense in the boundary, with arbitrary prescribed tails at the grafting sites. Every result below has been formally verified in Lean~4. We close with several optimisation questions and a higher-codimension variant.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26129

    📖 中文摘要

    设U均匀分布在单位球面上。我们为三维凸体的随机宽度w_K(U)和亮度b_K(U)发展自包含的正逆理论。对于每个满维多面体，全局球面余面积公式将宽度密度表示为有限角和，由其余体的法扇决定；特别地，密度是分段实解析的，具有有限几何确定的临界集。该理论给出正四面体宽度的精确密度，回答了Finch的一个问题，以及正截角八面体的精确密度，连同四面体亮度律和等价的菱形十二面体宽度律。在逆问题方面，二阶和三阶极化余弦变换矩重构有限标记方向系统，只要观测三角形张成相关图的循环空间；符号图切换描述不可避免的模糊性。相比之下，相等的三维内在体积不决定宽度律或亮度律，即使对于中心对称体。移除空间秩约束给出中心多元折叠正态向量的无维可识别性定理：成对绝对矩和锚定族的三重绝对矩，包括完全相关图的|m-1|^2个标记观测，确定相关矩阵直到对角符号共轭，无需四阶矩。调和分解进一步将二次方差贡献识别为加权框架算子无迹部分的平方Frobenius范数的常数倍，并解释为何该贡献在不可约对称下消失。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let U be uniformly distributed on the unit sphere. We develop a self-contained forward and inverse theory for the random width w_K(U) and brightness b_K(U) of three-dimensional convex bodies. For every full-dimensional polytope, a global spherical co-area formula expresses the width density as a finite sum of angular apertures determined by the normal fan of its difference body; in particular, the density is piecewise real analytic with a finite geometrically determined critical set. This theory yields exact densities for the width of the regular tetrahedron, resolving a question of Finch, and for the regular truncated octahedron, together with the tetrahedral brightness law and the equivalent rhombic-dodecahedral width law. On the inverse side, second- and third-order polarized cosine-transform moments reconstruct finite labelled direction systems whenever the observed triangles span the cycle space of the correlation graph; signed-graph switching describes the unavoidable ambiguity. In contrast, equal three-dimensional intrinsic volumes do not determine either the width law or the brightness law, even for centrally symmetric bodies. Removing the spatial rank constraint gives a dimension-free identifiability theorem for centered multivariate folded-normal vectors: pairwise absolute moments and an anchored family of triple absolute moments, comprising |m - 1|^2 labelled observations for a complete correlation graph, determine the correlation matrix up to diagonal sign conjugacy without fourth-order moments. A harmonic decomposition further identifies the degree-two variance contribution as a constant multiple of the squared Frobenius norm of the traceless part of the weighted frame operator and explains why this contribution vanishes under irreducible symmetry.

  9. [2609.26262] 几何数据集的紧屏幕与锥形紧化
    Compact Screens and Pyramidal Compactification of Geometric Data Sets
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 引入可观测距离以解决经典距离的不可分性。
    • 新距离使几何数据集类可分且测地。
    • 构造紧屏幕和锥形紧化。
    • 收敛由有限测量层的Box-Hausdorff行为检测。
    • 并证明与公共因子的和度量积是非扩张的。
    💡 引入可观测距离使几何数据集类可分且测地，构造紧屏幕和锥形紧化。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们引入一个可观测距离，通过固定有界坐标比较实值特征。该坐标保留有限特征值之间的区别，同时压缩它们独立逃逸到无穷，这是所有几何数据集上经典可观测距离不可分的根源。新距离使全类可分且测地：每对由常速路径连接，在度量测度空间上诱导拓扑与集中拓扑一致。从同一坐标我们构造紧屏幕，其特征取值于一个固定紧区间。屏幕类对于Box距离是波兰且测地的。通过特征顺序组织其有限特征商，得到一个紧锥空间。由单个紧屏幕生成的锥形成稠密子空间，因此该锥空间在过渡到紧屏幕后紧化原始类。收敛通过每个有限测量层的Box-Hausdorff行为检测。最后，与公共度量测度因子取和度量积对新距离是非扩张的。更精确地说，由原始空间的规定耦合和公共因子的对角耦合确定的比较被保留，而在所有乘积耦合上的优化给出非扩张不等式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce an observable distance that compares realvalued features through a fixed bounded coordinate. The coordinate retains the distinction between finite feature values while compressing their independent escape to infinity, the source of nonseparability for the classical observable distance on all geometric data sets. The new distance makes the full class separable and geodesic: every pair is joined by a constant-speed path, and on metric measure spaces the induced topology agrees with the concentration topology. From the same coordinate we construct compact screens, whose features take values in one fixed compact interval. The screened class is Polish and geodesic for the Box distance. Organizing its finite-feature quotients by the feature order yields a compact pyramid space. Pyramids generated by single compact screens form a dense subspace, so this pyramid space compactifies the original class after passage to compact screens. Convergence is detected through the Box-Hausdorff behavior of every finite measurement layer. Finally, taking sum-metric products with a common metric measure factor is nonexpansive for the new distance. More precisely, the comparison determined by a prescribed coupling of the original spaces and the diagonal coupling of the common factor is preserved, whereas optimization over all product couplings yields the nonexpansive inequality.

  10. [2609.26335] 标记包装测度与容量的覆盖构造
    A covering construction of labelled packing measure and content
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 通过覆盖构造引入标记包装测度。
    • 证明其与经典包装测度有相同零集和临界指数。
    • 定义标记包装容量并在特定柱集上一致。
    • 应用于自相似吸引子和图有向系统。
    • 无需分离假设。
    💡 通过覆盖构造引入标记包装测度，证明其与经典包装测度有相同零集和临界指数。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们通过覆盖构造引入标记包装测度，其中每个集合有一个独立正标记，限制其直径和局部包装成本中的半径。让标记趋于零定义一个度量外测度M^s。对于每个s>0和每个度量空间(X,d)，我们证明2^{-s}P^s(F) ≤ M^s(F) ≤ C(s)P^s(F)（F⊂X），其中P^s(F)是经典包装测度，C(s)仅依赖于s。因此标记包装测度和经典包装测度有相同的零集和临界指数。允许任意有限正标记定义标记包装容量。在公共正Hausdorff和包装维数处，该容量和标记包装测度在不可约有限型子移位的柱集上一致。特别地，对于非单点自相似吸引子和有限强连通图有向系统的分量，等式成立，无需分离假设。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce labelled packing measure by a covering construction in which each set has an independent positive label bounding its diameter and the radii in its local packing cost. Letting the labels tend to zero defines a metric outer measure $M^s$. For every $s>0$ and every metric space $(X,d)$, we prove \[ 2^{-s}{P}^s(F)\le{M}^s(F) \le C(s) P^s(F)\qquad(F\subset X), \] where $ P^s(F)$ is the classical packing measure and $C(s)$ depends only on $s$. Thus labelled packing measure and classical packing measure have the same null sets and critical exponent. Allowing arbitrary finite positive labels defines labelled packing content. At the common positive Hausdorff and packing dimension, this content and the labelled packing measure agree on cylinders associated with irreducible subshifts of finite type. In particular, equality holds for non-singleton self-similar attractors and components of finite strongly connected graph-directed systems, without separation assumptions.

    • 利用Berkovich仿射线实现路径连通扩张。
    • 制定梯度下降并有效学习模算术线性模型。
    • 演示动量和Adam变体及分类任务。
    • 解决Martins提出的开放问题。
    💡 首次实现p进参数机器学习模型的连续梯度下降，利用Berkovich仿射线进行优化。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们提出第一个用于具有p进参数的机器学习模型的原生连续梯度下降方法。现有的原生优化器是离散的，主要是组合搜索，因为p进数Q_p是完全不连通的，标准损失在最小值外是平坦的。为了实现连续优化，我们提出通过其Berkovich仿射线处理Q_p：这是Q_p的规范路径连通扩张，保持其等距并唯一扩展其解析映射。该壳是度量树，具有可解释点和局部导数，我们证明这能实现有效的优化器和反向传播。我们制定梯度下降并证明其近似能有效学习Q_p系数的线性模型以进行模算术，这是一种类似XOR的任务，不能用R上的线性模型表达。我们还演示了动量和Adam变体、线性回归以及二进制编码层次（Quillian语义网络）上的分类，解决了Martins（2025）提出的开放问题。库见https://github.com/google-deepmind/padic-ml。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We present the first method for native, continuous gradient descent for machine learning models with $p$-adic parameters. Existing native optimizers are discrete, mostly combinatorial searches, as the $p$-adic numbers $\mathbb{Q}_p$ are totally disconnected, with standard losses that are flat away from their minima. To enable continuous optimization, we propose working with $\mathbb{Q}_p$ via its Berkovich affine line: a canonical, path-connected expansion of $\mathbb{Q}_p$ that preserves its isometries and uniquely extends its analytic maps. This hull is a metric tree with interpretable points and local derivatives, which we show enables effective optimizers and backpropagation. We formulate gradient descent and show that its approximations efficiently learn linear models with coefficients in $\mathbb{Q}_p$ to do modular arithmetic, an XOR-like task not expressible by linear models in $\mathbb{R}$. We also demonstrate momentum and Adam variants, linear regression, and classification on binary-encoded hierarchies (Quillian semantic networks), addressing open problems posed by Martins (2025). Library at https://github.com/google-deepmind/padic-ml

  13. [2609.26286] 复Banach等距猜想
    The Complex Banach Isometric Conjecture
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究复Banach等距猜想。
    • 通过余维一约化，问题转化为刻画复超平面截面等价的凸体。
    • 证明这样的体是Hermitian椭球。
    • 利用丛-度方法和Brouwer度推出范数多项式性质。
    • 最终由平行四边形恒等式得到一般复Banach空间结论。
    💡 证明复Banach等距猜想：若某固定维数所有子空间线性等距，则空间为Hilbert空间。
    📖 中文摘要

    Banach问，如果一个赋范空间对于某个固定维数大于一，所有该维子空间都线性等距，那么它是否必须是Hilbert空间。我们证明复情形。通过余维一约化，本质有限维问题是刻画复(n+1)空间中的平衡凸体，其复超平面截面都复线性等价。我们证明这样的体是Hermitian椭球。证明改编了Lu和Yang最近用于实问题的丛-度方法，具有复设置特有的两个特征。协变归一化后，写G=Aut_C(S)为模型截面S的复线性对称群。精确截面映射形成S^{2n+1}上的主G-丛；约化到G^∘将其障碍置于π_{2n}(G^∘)，这是有限的。通过适当正度自映射拉回使该丛平凡化，并产生精确复线性截面映射的全局Lipschitz族。Brouwer度和带符号度公式然后推出p_S^{2n+2}和p_S^{2n+4}是实齐次多项式，其中p_S是模型截面的范数。唯一分解迫使p_S^2是二次的，相位不变性使所得二次形式为Hermitian。平行四边形恒等式然后给出一般复Banach空间陈述。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Banach asked whether a normed space must be Hilbert if, for one fixed dimension greater than one, all subspaces of that dimension are linearly isometric. We prove the complex case. By the codimension-one reduction, the essential finite-dimensional problem is to characterize a balanced convex body in a complex $(n+1)$-space whose complex hyperplane sections are all complex-linearly equivalent. We show that such a body is a Hermitian ellipsoid. The proof adapts the recent bundle--degree method of Lu and Yang for the real problem, with two features specific to the complex setting. After covariance normalization, write $G=\operatorname{Aut}{\mathbb C}(S)$ for the complex-linear symmetry group of a model section $S$. The exact section maps form a principal $G$-bundle over $S^{2n+1}$; reduction to $G^{\circ}$ places its obstruction in $\pi{2n}(G^{\circ})$, which is finite. Pulling back by a suitable positive-degree self-map trivializes this bundle and yields a global Lipschitz family of exact complex-linear section maps. Brouwer degree and a signed degree formula then imply that $p_S^{2n+2}$ and $p_S^{2n+4}$ are real homogeneous polynomials, where $p_S$ is the norm of a model section. Unique factorization forces $p_S^2$ to be quadratic, and phase invariance makes the resulting quadratic form Hermitian. The parallelogram identity then yields the general complex Banach-space statement.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26286

    📖 中文摘要

    曲线的p-模、测试计划、上梯度、图卡、微分、能量近似和方向密度都是与度量测度空间中Sobolev函数理论相关的概念。本文的目的是为Lipschitz函数和Weaver导数给出类似的几何和“片段式”理论，其中曲线片段的∞-模、*-上梯度和Alberti表示起核心作用。我们给出片段式图卡的新定义，并证明它们对于有限Hausdorff维数的空间存在。我们用Alberti表示和∞-模给出p-对偶的替代，并呈现*-上梯度理论。此外，我们给出Lipschitz函数近似的新且更尖锐的结果，这产生方向密度。我们的结果适用于所有完备可分度量测度空间。在此过程中，我们证明Sobolev世界和Lipschitz世界之间有强烈的平行性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The $p$-modulus of curves, test plans, upper gradients, charts, differentials, approximations in energy and density of directions are all concepts associated to the theory of Sobolev functions in metric measure spaces. The purpose of this paper is to give an analogous geometric and ``fragment-wise'' theory for Lipschitz functions and Weaver derivations, where $\infty$-modulus of curve fragments, $\ast$-upper gradients and Alberti representations play a central role. We give a new definition of fragment-wise charts and prove that they exists for spaces with finite Hausdorff dimension. We give a replacement for $p$-duality in terms of Alberti representations and $\infty$-modulus and present the theory of $\ast$-upper gradients. Further, we give new and sharper results for approximations of Lipschitz functions, which yields the density of directions. Our results are applicable to all complete and separable metric measure spaces. In the process, we show that there are strong parallels between the Sobolev and Lipschitz worlds.
