    📖 中文摘要

    自Isaac Newton于1694年首次研究亲吻数问题以来，确定中心球周围非重叠球的最大数量一直是离散几何的定义性挑战。作为Hilbert第18问题的局部类比，它在几何、数论和信息论中具有深远影响。尽管格和码取得了重大进展，但该领域局限于孤立的极值配置，掩盖了底层几何原理。这里我们将对象转移到更广泛的极值配置空间，从而为亲吻数问题开辟新路径。因此，我们将此问题重新表述为合作矩阵补全博弈，并训练强化学习系统PackingStar来解决它。一个玩家填充余弦条目，另一个纠正次优条目，使爆炸性几何复杂性变得可处理。在极值配置空间中，PackingStar发现新的可解释几何结构，改进了15个保持数十年的强界及其推广，其中几个在自然内积下可证明最优。这些发现揭示了Fischer群Fi22的第一个显式球码实现，扩展了子群结构的经典欧几里得表示，并直接启发了数学家的后续突破。总体而言，这项工作提供了AI驱动在Hilbert级别问题上进展的早期例子，展示了强化学习如何通过解锁更具表现力的对象来推进数学发现。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Since Isaac Newton first studied the Kissing Number Problem in 1694, determining the maximal number of non-overlapping spheres around a central sphere has remained a defining challenge in discrete geometry. As the local analogue of Hilbert's 18th problem, it has profound implications across geometry, number theory and information theory. Although lattices and codes have achieved significant progress, the field is confined to isolated extremal configurations, leaving underlying geometric principles obscured. Here we shift the object to the broader extremal configuration space, thereby opening a new path for the Kissing Number Problem. Accordingly, we recast this problem as a cooperative matrix-completion game, and train a reinforcement learning system, PackingStar, to solve it. One player fills cosine entries while the other corrects suboptimal ones, making explosive geometric complexity tractable. Working within extremal configuration spaces, PackingStar discovers new interpretable geometric structures that improve 15 strong bounds held for decades in kissing numbers and their generalizations, several of them provably optimal under natural inner products. These findings reveal the first explicit spherical-code realization of the Fischer group Fi22, extend the classical Euclidean representation of subgroup structure, and directly inspire subsequent breakthroughs by mathematicians. Overall, the work provides an early example of AI-driven progress on a Hilbert-calibre problem, showing how reinforcement learning advances mathematical discovery by unlocking more expressive objects.

  1. [2609.25393] 关于小素数生成乘法群
    On the generation of multiplicative groups by small primes
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究用指数0或1的小素数生成模q乘法群所需素数数目。
    • 证明存在(\log Q)^{1+ε}个素数生成几乎所有模q乘法群。
    • 素数大小至多(\log Q)^{C_*(A+1)}，指数1+ε最优。
    💡 证明用(\log Q)^{1+ε}个小素数可生成几乎所有模q乘法群，指数最优。
    📖 中文摘要

    受Regev关于其改进量子因子分解算法的问题启发，我们研究当每个素数只能以指数0或1使用时，需要多少个小素数来生成群(Z/qZ)^×。我们证明，对于每个固定ε>0和A>0，存在绝对常数C_*和至多(\log Q)^{1+ε}个素数，所有素数至多(\log Q)^{C_*(A+1)}，使得对于所有但O(Q(\log Q)^{-A})个整数q≤Q（隐含常数仅依赖于ε和A），(Z/qZ)^×的每个元素都是这些素数模q的某个子集的乘积。素数数目的指数1+ε在任意ε的意义下是最优的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Motivated by a question of Regev arising from his improved quantum factoring algorithm, we study how many small primes are needed to generate the group $({\mathbb Z}/q{\mathbb Z})^\times$ when each prime may be used with exponent only $0$ or $1$. We prove that, for every fixed $\varepsilon>0$ and $A>0$, there is an absolute constant $C_*$ and a set of at most $(\log Q)^{1+\varepsilon}$ primes, all at most $(\log Q)^{C_*(A+1)}$, such that for all but $O(Q(\log Q)^{-A})$ (with the implied constant depending only on $\varepsilon$ and $A$) integers $q\leq Q$, every element of $({\mathbb Z}/q{\mathbb Z})^\times$ is a product of a subset of these primes modulo $q$. The exponent $1+\varepsilon$ in the number of primes is best possible up to the arbitrary $\varepsilon$ in the exponent.

    📖 中文摘要

    阶为k、维数为n的魔线超立方体（或简称魔超立方体）是将数字1,…,k^n排列在k×…×k（n重）网格中，使得平行于坐标轴的每条k个数字的线具有相同的魔和。虽然这样的超立方体对每个阶k≥3和每个维数n都存在，但2阶魔线超立方体在任何维数n≥2中都不存在。对于2阶超立方体，我们因此将魔条件从线放宽到二维面或平面。我们将数字1,…,2^n排列在2×…×2（n重）网格中称为魔面的，如果每个2×2面具有相同的魔和。我们证明2阶魔面超立方体在每个维数n≥0中存在，并且当n为偶数时在某个自然变换集（大小为2^n(n+1)!）下唯一，当n为奇数时大小为2^n n·n!。作为应用，我们恢复并推广了经典的4×4 Khajuraho幻方，回答了Coxeter关于作用在384个“最完美”4×4幻方上的群的问题，并将图景扩展到更高维。特别地，我们证明在维数n中，这些最完美对象在Weyl群W(B_{2n})的某个自然作用下形成单个轨道。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A $\textit{magic-lined hypercube}$ (or, simply, $\textit{magic hypercube}$) of order $k$ and dimension $n$ is an arrangement of the numbers $1,\dots,k^n$ in a $k\times\cdots\times k$ ($n$-fold) grid such that every line of $k$ numbers parallel to a coordinate axis has the same magic sum. While such hypercubes exist for every order $k\ge 3$ and every dimension $n$, no magic-lined hypercube of order $2$ exists in any dimension $n\ge 2$. For hypercubes of order $2$, we thus relax the magic condition from lines to two-dimensional faces or planes. We call an arrangement of the numbers $1,\dots,2^n$ in a $2\times\cdots\times2$ ($n$-fold) grid \emph{magic-faced} if every $2\times2$ face has the same magic sum. We prove that a magic-faced hypercube of order $2$ exists in every dimension $n\ge0$, and that it is unique up to a certain natural set of transformations of size $2^n(n+1)!$ when $n$ is even and $2^n n\cdot n!$ when $n$ is odd. As an application, we recover and generalize the classical $4\times4$ Khajuraho magic square, answer a question of Coxeter on the group acting on the $384$ ``most-perfect" $4\times4$ magic squares, and extend the picture to higher dimensions. In particular, we prove that, in dimension $n$, these most-perfect objects form a single orbit under a certain natural action of the Weyl group $W(B_{2n})$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26762

  4. [2609.25104] 形式为2^n p^m+1（奇数指数m）的Carmichael数：Florian Luca定理的改进、一般独立性引理与计算研究
    Carmichael numbers of the form $2^n p^m+1$ with odd exponent $m$: refinements of a theorem of Florian Luca, a general independence lemma, and a computational study
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 扩展Luca关于2^n p^m+1型Carmichael数的定理。
    • 给出显式定量界和素因子个数界。
    • 将乘法独立性步骤推广到任意偶基。
    • 进行穷举计算验证小指数情形。
    • 结果回答了Luca的有限性问题并给出具体例子。
    💡 扩展Luca关于2^n p^m+1型Carmichael数的定理，给出定量界、一般独立性引理和计算验证。
    📖 中文摘要

    Florian Luca将他生命的最后一篇论文献给了奇数正整数k的集合K，使得2^n k+1对某个正整数n是Carmichael数。他证明如果m≥5固定且为奇数，则只有有限多个素数p使得p^m∈K。在这篇作为对他纪念的小注中，我们仅以他发表的工作为参考基础，从三个方向扩展他的结果。首先，我们从他的论证中提取显式定量界：每个奇数m的Carmichael数N=2^n p^m+1满足n<2^{2·10^7(m+1)^2m^2(log p)^2}，如果p>m则更尖锐的界n<25m^4 log p成立；对于p>500m^5 log m，我们进一步记录一致界ω(N)≤26m^7关于素因子个数。作为应用，这样的Carmichael数到x的数量为O_m(x^{1/m})。其次，我们将Luca证明中的乘法独立性步骤从基2推广到任意偶基b：如果b为偶数，m≥3为奇数且与b互素，p>m为素数且N=b^n p^m+1是Carmichael数，则当b^a p^β+1是N的素因子时，b^n p^m和b^a p^β乘法独立。第三，我们报告穷举计算，表明≤10^{18}的唯一奇数3≤m≤37的Carmichael数2^n p^m+1是1729=2^6·3^3+1，不存在奇数5≤m≤37的这样的数（对5≤m≤15扩展到10^{21}），且偶数4≤m≤36的唯一例子是46657=2^6·3^6+1=13·37·97，其中3^6=27^2是K的最小元素的平方。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Florian Luca devoted the last paper of his life to the set $\mathcal K$ of odd positive integers $k$ such that $2^n k+1$ is a Carmichael number for some positive integer $n$. He proved that if $m\ge 5$ is fixed and odd, then there are only finitely many primes $p$ with $p^m\in\mathcal K$. In this note, written as a small tribute to his memory, we extend his result in three directions, using only his published work as our reference base. First, we extract from his arguments explicit quantitative bounds: every Carmichael number $N=2^n p^m+1$ with $m$ odd satisfies $n<2^{2\cdot10^7(m+1)^2m^2(\log p)^2}$, and if $p>m$ the much sharper bound $n<25m^4\log p$ holds; for $p>500m^5\log m$ we further record the uniform bounds $\omega(N)\le 26m^7$ on the number of prime factors. As an application, the number of such Carmichael numbers up to $x$ is $O_m(x^{1/m})$. Second, we generalize the multiplicative independence step of Luca's proof from base $2$ to an arbitrary even base $b$: if $b$ is even, $m\ge3$ is odd and coprime to $b$, $p>m$ is prime and $N=b^n p^m+1$ is Carmichael, then $b^n p^m$ and $b^a p^\beta$ are multiplicatively independent whenever $b^a p^\beta+1$ is a prime factor of $N$. Third, we report an exhaustive computation showing that the only Carmichael number $2^n p^m+1\le 10^{18}$ with odd $3\le m\le 37$ is $1729=2^6\cdot 3^3+1$, that no such number exists with odd $5\le m\le 37$ (extended to $10^{21}$ for $5\le m\le 15$), and that the only example with even $4\le m\le 36$ is $46657=2^6\cdot 3^6+1=13\cdot 37\cdot 97$, where $3^6=27^2$ is the square of the smallest element of $\mathcal K$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25104

    📖 中文摘要

    多重 zeta 值的李代数可实现为空间 $\operatorname{ARI}^{\operatorname{pol}}_{\underline{al}\ast \underline{il}}$，即其交换在相差一个常数 mould 下为 alternil 的 alternal moulds 构成的空间，配备 $ari$ 括号。本文研究更大的空间 $\operatorname{BARI}_{\underline{il},swap}^{\operatorname{pol}}$，即 alternil、swap-不变的双 moulds 构成的空间，猜想它是多重 $q$-zeta 值的李代数。我们为该空间上的李括号 $uri$ 提出显式公式。此外，我们证明李代数 $\operatorname{ARI}^{\operatorname{pol}}_{\underline{al}\ast \underline{il}}$ 嵌入到 $\operatorname{BARI}_{\underline{il},swap}^{\operatorname{pol}}$ 中，且 $ari$ 括号直接转化为 $uri$ 括号。最后，我们考察 $\operatorname{BARI}_{\underline{il},swap}^{\operatorname{pol}}$ 的 associated-depth 分次，推广了 $\operatorname{ARI}_{\underline{al}\ast \underline{il}}^{\operatorname{pol}}$ 的已知 depth-分次框架。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The Lie algebra of multiple zeta values is realized as the space $\operatorname{ARI}^{\operatorname{pol}}_{\underline{al}\ast \underline{il}}$ of alternal moulds whose swap is alternil up to a constant mould, equipped with the $ari$ bracket. In this paper, we study the larger space $\operatorname{BARI}_{\underline{il},swap}^{\operatorname{pol}}$ of alternil, swap-invariant bimoulds, which is conjecturally the Lie algebra for multiple $q$-zeta values. We propose an explicit formula for a Lie bracket $uri$ on this space. Moreover, we prove that the Lie algebra $\operatorname{ARI}^{\operatorname{pol}}_{\underline{al}\ast \underline{il}}$ embeds into $\operatorname{BARI}_{\underline{il},swap}^{\operatorname{pol}}$, with the $ari$ bracket translating directly into the $uri$ bracket. Finally, we examine the associated-depth graded of $\operatorname{BARI}_{\underline{il},swap}^{\operatorname{pol}}$, extending the known depth-graded setup for $\operatorname{ARI}_{\underline{al}\ast \underline{il}}^{\operatorname{pol}}$.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究 $\mathbb{F}_q[T]$ 上的首一不可约多项式，其非首项数字相对于任意多项式基 $b(T)$ 避开一个给定的禁止数字集。将数字集等同于环 $D=\mathbb{F}_q[T]/(b)$，我们在允许数字集的 Fourier 参数条件下得到此类不可约多项式数目的渐近公式。主项包含一个奇异级数，度量允许数字中单位的相对密度，而误差项由逐点与平均 Fourier 估计控制。作为推论，我们得到一个仅依赖于禁止集大小的通用判别准则，以及针对结构化限制的更强结果，包括禁止集包含正比例数字的例子。特别地，我们处理加法陪集、与 $D$ 的中国剩余分解相容的限制以及逐系数限制。证明将限制系数的函数域圆法推广到任意多项式基。

    🌐 English Abstract

The main term contains a singular series measuring the relative density of units among the allowed digits, while the error term is controlled by both pointwise and averaged Fourier estimates. As a consequence, we obtain a general criterion depending only on the cardinality of the forbidden set, as well as stronger results for structured restrictions, including examples in which the forbidden set contains a positive proportion of all digits. In particular, we treat additive cosets, restrictions compatible with the Chinese remainder decomposition of $D$, and coefficient-wise restrictions. The proof adapts the function field circle method for restricted coefficients to arbitrary polynomial bases.

    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 t 表示为 n 的不同除数之和的自然密度 d_t。
    • 给出 d_t 的显式上下界，涉及 Erdős--Ford--Tenenbaum 常数。
    • 证明 d_t 不渐近于 K/log t，并得到点态超额下界。
    • 改进了 Erdős 的未指定界，并部分回答其问题。
    💡 给出 d_t 的显式上下界，证明 d_t 不渐近于 K/log t，并得到点态超额下界。
    📖 中文摘要

    对正整数 $t$，设 $d_t$ 表示 $t$ 可表示为 $n$ 的不同除数之和的 $n$ 的自然密度。Erdős 证明了 $d_t$ 存在，给出了未指定的多对数上界，未加证明地断言了匹配的下界，并问是否有 $d_t \sim c_3/(\log t)^{c_4}$。我们记录显式界 \[ \nu_t \le d_t \ll \frac{(\log\log t)^{\delta-3/2}}{(\log t)^{\delta}}, \qquad \nu_t = \frac{K}{\log t}\Bigl(1 + O\Bigl(\frac{\log\log t}{\log t}\Bigr)\Bigr), \] 其中 $\delta = 0.086071\ldots$ 是 Erdős--Ford--Tenenbaum 常数，$K=c\,e^{-\gamma}$，$c=1.33607\ldots$ 是 practical-number 常数。因此，若 Erdős 的渐近成立，则 $\delta<c_4\le 1$。一个双素数构造，使用半尺度 sumset 获得完全剩余覆盖，进而得到点态超额 \[ \liminf_{t\to\infty}(\log t)\,(d_t-\nu_t) \ge KI, \] 其中 $I=\int_0^2 G(w)\,\mathrm{d}w=0.05887\ldots$ 是显式初等积分。特别地，对每个充分大的 $t$ 有 $d_t\ge 0.79/\log t$，且 $d_t$ 不渐近于 $K/\log t$。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For a positive integer $t$, let $d_t$ denote the natural density of the set of $n$ for which $t$ is a sum of distinct divisors of $n$. Erd\H{o}s proved that $d_t$ exists, gave an unspecified polylogarithmic upper bound, asserted without proof a matching lower bound, and asked whether $d_t \sim c_3/(\log t)^{c_4}$. We record the explicit bounds \[ \nu_t \le d_t \ll \frac{(\log\log t)^{\delta-3/2}}{(\log t)^{\delta}}, \qquad \nu_t = \frac{K}{\log t}\Bigl(1 + O\Bigl(\frac{\log\log t}{\log t}\Bigr)\Bigr), \] where $\delta = 0.086071\ldots$ is the Erd\H{o}s--Ford--Tenenbaum constant, $K=c\,e^{-\gamma}$, and $c=1.33607\ldots$ is the practical-number constant. Consequently, if Erd\H{o}s's asymptotic holds, then $\delta<c_4\le 1$. A two-prime construction, using a half-scale sumset to obtain full residue coverage, then yields the pointwise excess \[ \liminf_{t\to\infty}(\log t)\,(d_t-\nu_t) \ge KI, \] where $I=\int_0^2 G(w)\,\mathrm{d}w=0.05887\ldots$ is an explicit elementary integral. In particular $d_t\ge 0.79/\log t$ for every sufficiently large $t$, and $d_t$ is not asymptotic to $K/\log t$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25446
  14. [2609.25457] 无幂格点中大洞的密度
    Density of large holes among power-free lattice points
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究无幂格点图中大洞的密度，即 R-深点及其代表点的密度。
    • 对任意 ℓ_q 范数，给出密度的一致渐近公式。
    • 结果改进了 Baake-Moody-Pleasants 和 Pleasants-Huck 的洞构造与上界。
    • 一维情形改进了 Grimmett 关于 r-free 整数间隙的渐近。
    💡 计算 R-深点及代表点的密度，给出精确渐近，并改进一维 r-free 间隙结果。
    📖 中文摘要

    对固定整数 $d,r\ge1$ 且 $dr\ge2$，$\mathbb{Z}^d$ 的 $r$-free 点是指坐标 gcd 不被任何素数的 $r$ 次幂整除的向量。我们考虑 $\mathbb{Z}^d$ 中非 $r$-free 点集 $W_{d,r}$ 作为最近邻邻接图的顶点集。对 $1\le q\le\infty$，称点 $t\in\mathbb Z^d$ 是 $R$-深的，若 $t+\{n\in\mathbb Z^d:\|n\|_q\le R\}\subseteq W_{d,r}$。因此 $R$-深点是闭 $\ell_q$-球的格中心，其格点全为非 $r$-free。称连通分支是 $R$-大的，若它包含一个 $R$-深点。我们通过选择字典序最小的 $R$-深点作为代表来标记每个有限 $R$-大分支。对 $1\le q\le\infty$ 一致地，我们证明 $R$-深点的密度与这些代表的密度均等于 $$ \exp\!\left\{ -\frac{v_{d,q}}{\zeta(dr)} \left(d(dr-1)R^d\log R+drR^d\log\log R\right) +O_{d,r}(R^d) \right\} $$ 当 $R\to\infty$，其中 $v_{d,q}$ 表示 $(\mathbb R^d,\|\cdot\|_q)$ 中单位球的体积。对深点，这改进了 Baake、Moody 和 Pleasants 以及 Pleasants 和 Huck 的正密度洞构造，以及后者对稀疏模式频率的上界。当 $d=1$ 时，我们证明 $r$-free 整数后跟恰好 $g-1$ 个非 $r$-free 整数再跟一个 $r$-free 整数的密度为 $$ \exp\!\left\{-\frac{r-1}{\zeta(r)}g\log g -\frac{r}{\zeta(r)}g\log\log g+O_r(g)\right\} $$ 当 $g\to\infty$，改进了由 Grimmett 工作得到的渐近 $\exp\left\{-\left(\frac{r-1}{\zeta(r)}+o(1)\right) g\log g\right\}$。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For fixed integers $d,r\ge1$ with $dr\ge2$, the $r$-free points of $\mathbb{Z}^d$ are the vectors whose coordinate gcd is not divisible by the $r$th power of any prime. We consider the set $W_{d,r}$ of non-$r$-free points in $\mathbb{Z}^d$ as the vertex set of a graph with nearest-neighbour adjacency. For $1\le q\le\infty$, we say that a point $t\in\mathbb Z^d$ is $R$-deep if $t+\{n\in\mathbb Z^d:\|n\|_q\le R\}\subseteq W_{d,r}$. Thus $R$-deep points are the lattice centres of closed $\ell_q$-balls whose lattice points are all non-$r$-free. We call a connected component $R$-large if it contains an $R$-deep point. We mark each finite $R$-large component by selecting its lexicographically least $R$-deep point as its representative. Uniformly for $1\le q\le\infty$, we show that the density of $R$-deep points and the density of these representatives both equal $$ \exp\!\left\{ -\frac{v_{d,q}}{\zeta(dr)} \left(d(dr-1)R^d\log R+drR^d\log\log R\right) +O_{d,r}(R^d) \right\} $$ as $R\to\infty$, where $v_{d,q}$ denotes the volume of the unit ball in $(\mathbb R^d,\|\cdot\|_q)$. For deep points, this sharpens the positive-density hole constructions of Baake, Moody, and Pleasants and of Pleasants and Huck, and the latter authors' upper bounds for sparse-pattern frequencies. When $d=1$, we show that the density of $r$-free integers followed by exactly $g-1$ non-$r$-free integers and then another $r$-free integer is $$\exp\!\left\{-\frac{r-1}{\zeta(r)}g\log g -\frac{r}{\zeta(r)}g\log\log g+O_r(g)\right\}$$ as $g\to\infty$, improving the asymptotic $\exp\left\{-\left(\frac{r-1}{\zeta(r)}+o(1)\right) g\log g\right\}$ which follows from Grimmett's work. % For fixed $r$, we also obtain estimates uniform in growing dimensions $d=O_r((\log R)^r)$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25457
  15. [2609.25628] Möbius 函数的一个惊人推广
    A surprising generalization of the M\"{o}bius function
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 推广 Spencer-Brown 的 Möbius 函数递推公式。
    • 用任意算术函数替换最大整数函数，定义新函数族 ⋆(n)。
    • 证明 ⋆(p) = -1 当且仅当 p 为素数。
    • 给出素数的代数刻画，揭示除数和的深层结构。
    💡 推广 Spencer-Brown 的 Möbius 递推公式，证明新函数在素数处取 -1 当且仅当为素数。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们引入 George Spencer-Brown 给出的 Möbius 函数递推公式 $\mu(n) = 1-n-\sum_{d=2}^{n-1}\mu(d)\left[\frac{n}{d}\right]$ 的一个广泛推广。通过将该经典递推中的最大整数函数 $\left[\frac{n}{d}\right]$ 替换为任意算术函数 $f\left(\left[\frac{n}{d}\right]\right)$ 且 $f(1)=1$，我们定义了一个新的广义函数族，记为 $\star(n)$。我们证明 $\star(p) = -1$ 当且仅当 $p$ 是素数。这一结果给出了素数的一个惊人的代数刻画，并揭示了除数和与最大整数函数背后的深层结构性质。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce a broad generalization of a recursive formula for the M\"{o}bius function $\mu(n) = 1-n-\sum_{d=2}^{n-1}\mu(d)\left[\frac{n}{d}\right]$ due to George Spencer-Brown. By replacing the greatest integer function $\left[\frac{n}{d}\right]$ in this classical recurrence with an arbitrary arithmetic function $f\left(\left[\frac{n}{d}\right]\right)$ with $f(1)=1$, we define a new generalized family of functions, denoted $\star(n)$. We prove that $\star(p) = -1$ if and only if $p$ is prime. This result yields a surprising algebraic characterization of primes and reveals a deep structural property underlying divisor sums and the greatest integer function.

  17. [2609.25739] 截断五边形数级数的正性与 Merca 的一些猜想
    On the positivity of truncated pentagonal number series and some conjectures of Merca
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 Merca 关于加权截断五边形数级数非负性的四个猜想。
    • 将指数映射视为动力系统，利用轨道望远镜技术。
    • 证明三条轨道不交且望远镜化为简单乘积。
    • 得到级数分解为非负系数级数，完成猜想证明。
    • 方法补充了经典的 Pólya-Szegő 准则。
    💡 证明 Merca 关于加权截断五边形数级数非负性的四个猜想，引入轨道望远镜方法。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 $\nu_2(m)$ 表示正整数 $m$ 的 $2$-adic 赋值，并令 $N_m=m\bigl(1+\nu_2(m)/2\bigr)$。我们证明 Merca 关于由无穷乘积 $\prod_{m\ge 1}(1-q^{2N_m})$ 和 $\prod_{r\ge 1}\bigl(q^{2^r r};q^{2^{r+1}r}\bigr)_\infty$ 加权的截断五边形数级数非负性的四个猜想。我们的方法是将指数映射 $m\mapsto m(\nu_2(m)+2)$ 视为正整数上的动力系统：相关商的因子沿其前向轨道链接成链，且从 $1$、$4$ 和 $5$ 播种的三条轨道两两不交并望远镜化为恰好 $1/\bigl((1-q)(1-q^4)(1-q^5)\bigr)$。指数三元组 $(1,4,5)$ 在 Liu 早期工作的意义下是可容许的，这给出了所需分解为两个具有非负系数的级数。这种轨道望远镜技术似乎是 Pólya--Szegő 准则的一种补充机制，后者是文献中大多数此类正性结果的基础。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\nu_2(m)$ denote the $2$-adic valuation of a positive integer $m$ and set $N_m=m\bigl(1+\nu_2(m)/2\bigr)$. We prove four conjectures of Merca on the nonnegativity of truncated pentagonal number series weighted by the infinite products $\prod_{m\ge 1}(1-q^{2N_m})$ and $\prod_{r\ge 1}\bigl(q^{2^r r};q^{2^{r+1}r}\bigr)_\infty$. Our method is to regard the exponent map $m\mapsto m(\nu_2(m)+2)$ as a dynamical system on the positive integers: the factors of the associated quotient link into chains along its forward orbits, and the three orbits seeded at $1$, $4$ and $5$ are pairwise disjoint and telescope to exactly $1/\bigl((1-q)(1-q^4)(1-q^5)\bigr)$. The exponent triple $(1,4,5)$ is admissible in the sense of earlier work by Liu, which yields the desired factorization into two series with nonnegative coefficients. This orbit telescoping technique appears to be a mechanism complementary to the P\'olya--Szeg\H o criterion that underlies most existing positivity results of this kind in the literature.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25739

For $\psi:\mathbb N\to\mathbb{R}_{\ge0}$ satisfying $\psi(n)\to \infty$ and $\alpha, \beta\in [0, \infty]$, define the joint level set \[F_{\alpha,\beta}:=\Big\{x\in [0,1)\colon \liminf_{n\to\infty}\frac{\log (a_{n+1}(x))}{\psi(n)}=\alpha,\ \limsup_{n\to\infty}\frac{\log (a_{n+1}(x))}{\log q_n(x)}=\beta\Big\}. \] We determine the Hausdorff dimension of $F_{\alpha,\beta}$ for all values of $ \alpha$ and $\beta$.   23. [2609.26014] 相等子集和与接近的除数
    Equal subset sums and close divisors
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究相等子集和与接近除数的关系，定义 α_k 和 β_k。
    • 证明 α_k = β_k/(1-β_k)，解决 Ford-Green-Koukoulopoulos 的猜想。
    • 证明弱熵阈值与严格熵阈值重合。
    • 方法结合旗细化、熵凹性和一致近似子集和上界。
    💡 证明 α_k = β_k/(1-β_k)，解决 Ford-Green-Koukoulopoulos 的猜想，并证明熵阈值重合。
    📖 中文摘要

    对 $k\geq2$，设 $\alpha_k$ 为指数 $a$ 的上确界，使得几乎所有整数 $n$ 在相对长度 $(\log n)^{-a}$ 的乘法区间内有 $k$ 个不同除数。独立地以概率 $1/i$ 选择每个正整数 $i$，形成随机集 $\mathbf A$，设 $\beta_k$ 为 $c<1$ 的上确界，使得当 $D\to\infty$ 时，集合 $\mathbf A\cap[D^c,D]$ 以趋于 1 的概率有 $k$ 个具有相同和的不同子集。我们证明 $\alpha_k=\beta_k/(1-\beta_k)$，解决了 Ford、Green 和 Koukoulopoulos [Invent. Math. 232 (2023), 1027--1160] 的一个猜想。我们还证明他们的弱熵阈值与严格熵阈值重合。证明结合了旗细化与熵凹性，以及一个对任意平移一致的近似子集和上界。一个具有独立几何素数指数的模型随后将该界转移到除数。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For $k\geq2$, let $\alpha_k$ be the supremum of the exponents $a$ for which almost every integer $n$ has $k$ distinct divisors in a multiplicative interval of relative length $(\log n)^{-a}$. Select each positive integer $i$ independently with probability $1/i$, forming a random set $\mathbf A$, and let $\beta_k$ be the supremum of the $c<1$ for which, with probability tending to one as $D\to\infty$, the set $\mathbf A\cap[D^c,D]$ has $k$ distinct subsets with the same sum. We prove that $\alpha_k=\beta_k/(1-\beta_k)$, resolving a conjecture of Ford, Green and Koukoulopoulos [Invent. Math. 232 (2023), 1027--1160]. We also prove that their weak and strict entropy thresholds coincide. The proof combines flag refinement and entropy concavity with an upper bound for approximate subset sums that is uniform in arbitrary translations. A model with independent geometric prime exponents then transfers this bound to divisors.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26014

    Boyd's conductor-11 Mahler measure conjecture: proof of the split-integral identity (C3), with an exact structural analysis of the family S_k
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 证明 Boyd 关于导子 11 的第三个 Mahler 测度恒等式 (C3)。
    • 将分裂积分与 regulator 积分等同，证明其同调类为 2γ^-。
    • 通过 Brunault 的 Siegel 单位公式计算 regulator，得到 I_split = b_11。
    • 分析族 S_k 的环面交点和模单位情形，并记录进一步猜想。
    • 所有计算在区间算术中验证，数值吻合到 366 位。
    💡 证明 Boyd 导子-11 的第三个 Mahler 测度恒等式 (C3)，并分析族 S_k 的结构。
    📖 中文摘要

    Boyd 1998 年关于 Mahler 测度与椭圆曲线 L-值之间猜想恒等式 $m(P)=r\,|L'(E,0)|$ 的表格始于最小可能导子 $N=11$。第三个导子-11 恒等式 (C3) 涉及多项式 $S_0=y^2+(x^2+1)y+x^3$，它在单位环面上消失，并断言 $\log|y|$ 围绕分支割的有符号分裂积分 $I_{\mathrm{split}}$ 等于 $b_{11}=L'(E_{11},0)$。我们证明 (C3)。证明将分裂积分等同于沿 Samart 的有符号开链 $\tilde\gamma$ 的 regulator 积分，通过小分支弧 $\beta_0$ 闭合成闭反不变循环 $C'$，并证明其同调类为 $2\gamma^-$，其中 $\gamma^-$ 生成 $H_1(E,\mathbb{Z})^-$：周期比是先验整数的，球算术 (Arb) 将其确定为 2。通过 Brunault 已证明的 Siegel 单位公式直接计算 regulator——符号 $\{x,y\}$ 是 $X_1(11)$ 上的一对模单位，因此不需要 Bloch 的 diamond 定理——然后得到 $I_{\mathrm{split}}=b_{11}$；符号在区间算术中验证，恒等式与数值值吻合到 366 位。对族 $S_k=y^2+(x^2+kx+1)y+x^3$，我们精确确定环面交点及模单位/温和情形；进一步 Boyd 型赋值记录为猜想。在 $k=-1$（导子 53）时机制可证明失效；附录条件性地处理 Samart 的导子-17 类似物。所有计算可从附带代码重现；每个验证步骤在区间算术内进行。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Boyd's 1998 tables of conjectural identities $m(P)=r\,|L'(E,0)|$ between Mahler measures of two-variable polynomials and $L$-values of elliptic curves begin with the smallest possible conductor, $N=11$. The third conductor-$11$ identity, (C3), concerns the polynomial $S_0=y^2+(x^2+1)y+x^3$, which vanishes on the unit torus, and asserts that a signed split integral $I_{\mathrm{split}}$ of $\log|y|$ around the branch cut equals $b_{11}=L'(E_{11},0)$. We prove (C3). The proof identifies the split integral with a regulator integral along Samart's signed open chain $\tilde\gamma$, closed by a small-branch arc $\beta_0$ into a closed anti-invariant cycle $C'$, and proves its homology class is $2\gamma^-$ with $\gamma^-$ generating $H_1(E,\mathbb{Z})^-$: the period ratio is a-priori integral, and ball arithmetic (Arb) pins it to $2$. A direct regulator computation via Brunault's proved Siegel-unit formula---the symbol $\{x,y\}$ being a pair of modular units on $X_1(11)$, so Bloch's diamond theorem is not needed---then yields $I_{\mathrm{split}}=b_{11}$; the sign is certified in interval arithmetic, and the identity agrees with the numerical value to $366$ digits. For the family $S_k=y^2+(x^2+kx+1)y+x^3$ we determine exactly the torus intersections and the modular-unit/tempered cases; further Boyd-type evaluations are recorded as conjectures. At $k=-1$ (conductor $53$) the mechanism provably fails; an appendix treats Samart's conductor-$17$ analogue conditionally. All computations are reproducible from the accompanying code; every certification step is carried out within interval arithmetic.

    📖 中文摘要

    🌐 English Abstract

    We axiomatize the differential-comparison continuation method of [Zf] into a general machine for proving Mahler measure identities at CM points: a modular parametrization with rigorous tail bounds, a single-valued holomorphic Mahler differential on the complement of the critical image, a propagation lemma replacing all monodromy and universal-cover arguments, interval-arithmetic path certification, and an exact three-track CM evaluation. Applied to Samart's family $n_4(s)=4\mathrm m(x^4+y^4+z^4+1+s^{1/4}xyz)$, the machine proves twelve further entries of his 2015 table [Sa15, Table 6], including the first identities in this family proved at non-degenerate CM points of class number two (attached to $\mathbb Q(\sqrt{-6})$ and $\mathbb Q(\sqrt{-15})$) and of class number four (attached to $\mathbb Q(\sqrt{-21})$, with four newforms and the genus group $(\mathbb Z/2)^2$). Every identity is certified by an exact algebraic track, rigorous final-identity interval locks (half-widths $4.6\times10^{-53}$ to $5.1\times10^{-52}$), and independent 50--60-digit cross-checks; all scripts are public. The evaluation track also yields six new identities at Heegner points ($D=11,19,27,43,67,163$), stated as conjectures with 60-digit evidence; we close with an umbrella conjecture organizing the proved and conjectured identities, and a precise analysis of the five remaining open entries.

    📖 中文摘要

    本文从装饰等边三角剖分的剥离的初等几何研究椭圆曲线的模性问题。剥离是一个有限展开域连同重建三角剖分曲面的边界识别。在亏格一中它恢复万有覆盖及其秩二 deck 格。对固定素数 $\ell$，有限无分歧覆盖 $[\ell^n]:E\to E$ 形成一个相容塔，其 deck 群为 $E[\ell^n]$；它们的逆极限是 Tate 模 $T_\ell(E)$，在 $\Q$ 上相容的 Galois 作用给出通常的 $\ell$-adic 表示。相比之下，循环同源给出指数 $q$ 的格邻居，形成 Hecke 和 Brandt 对应的局部模型。对素数导子 $p$ 的半稳定椭圆曲线 $E/\Q$，缺失的互反性是在不使用模性的情况下构造次数零超奇异 Brandt 模中的非零类 $c_E$，使得在每个好素数处 $B_qc_E=a_q(E)c_E$。我明确这一障碍并研究附于 Frobenius 判别式 $D_q=a_q(E)^2-4q$ 的有向 CM 包。新的初等结果表明 $D_q$ 模 $p$ 非平方恰好当剩余 Frobenius 多项式不可约，任何包含此类元素的剩余像给出正密度素数集使得 $p$ 在由 $D_q$ 确定的二次域中惰性，且相应的判别式必然无界。

    🌐 English Abstract

    This paper studies the modularity problem for elliptic curves from the elementary geometry of peels of decorated equilateral triangulations. A peel is a finite developing domain together with the boundary identifications that reconstruct the triangulated surface. In genus one it recovers the universal cover and its rank-two deck lattice. For a fixed prime $\ell$, the finite unramified coverings $[\ell^n]:E\to E$ form a compatible tower whose deck groups are $E[\ell^n]$; their inverse limit is the Tate module $T_\ell(E)$, and over $\Q$ the compatible Galois action gives the usual $\ell$-adic representation. Cyclic isogenies, by contrast, give the index-$q$ lattice neighbors that form the local model for Hecke and Brandt correspondences. For a semistable elliptic curve $E/\Q$ of prime conductor $p$, the missing reciprocity is the construction, without using modularity, of a nonzero class $c_E$ in the degree-zero supersingular Brandt module such that $B_qc_E=a_q(E)c_E$ at every good prime. I make this obstruction explicit and study oriented CM packets attached to the Frobenius discriminants $D_q=a_q(E)^2-4q$. New elementary results show that $D_q$ is a nonsquare modulo $p$ exactly when the residual Frobenius polynomial is irreducible, that any residual image containing such an element gives a positive-density set of primes for which $p$ is inert in the quadratic field determined by $D_q$, and that the corresponding discriminants are necessarily unbounded.

    我们研究随机p进正交矩阵模型的线性化。设A_n和B_n分别是Z_p上的Haar随机n×n特殊正交矩阵和交错矩阵。对于奇素数p，我们证明当n→∞且n取相同奇偶性的整数时，cok(A_n-I_n)和cok(B_n)具有相同的极限分布。这两个依赖于奇偶性的极限在行列式为-1的正交矩阵下互换，而整个正交群给出它们的等混合。对于p=2，线性化不保持极限余核分布，我们利用旋量范数明确确定了特殊正交模型的极限分布，该范数控制挠子群阶的2进赋值的奇偶性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the linearization of the random $p$-adic orthogonal matrix model. For an odd prime $p$, we prove that $\mathrm{cok}(A_n-I_n)$ and $\mathrm{cok}(B_n)$ have the same limiting distribution as $n \to \infty$ through integers of the same parity. The two parity-dependent limits are interchanged for orthogonal matrices of determinant $-1$, while the full orthogonal group gives their equal mixture. For $p=2$, linearization does not preserve the limiting cokernel distribution, and we explicitly determine the limiting distributions for the special orthogonal model in terms of the spinor norm, which controls the parity of the $2$-adic valuation of the order of the torsion subgroup.

    • 应用于A_N李代数的根格，与拓扑扭曲Yang-Mills理论相关。
    • 为高阶深度mock模形式提供了新的积分表示。
    💡 将Appell函数的非全纯完备化表示为迭代Eichler积分，并应用于A_N根格。
    📖 中文摘要

    Appell函数是一大类多变量拟椭圆函数，也是高阶深度mock模形式的实例。这些函数的一个核心方面是它们的非全纯模完备化。我们之前的工作为一般正定格Λ发展了Appell函数，并用广义误差函数M_P表示了非全纯完备化，其中M_P是Λ⊗C中P维超平面上的积分。作为续篇，我们将M_P表示为变量w_j∈H的P维迭代积分，使得Appell函数的完备化具有模形式的迭代Eichler积分形式。我们将其应用于A_N李代数的根格情形，该情形因出现在拓扑扭曲Yang-Mills理论的配分函数中而特别有趣。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Appell functions are a large class of multi-variable quasi-elliptic functions, which are also instances of higher depth mock modular forms. A central aspect of these functions is their non-holomorphic modular completion. Our previous work developed Appell functions for a general positive definite lattice $\Lambda$, and expressed the non-holomorphic completion in terms of the generalized error functions $M_P$, which are integrals over a $P$-dimensional hyperplane in $\Lambda\otimes \mathbb{C}$. In this sequel, we express $M_P$ as a $P$-dimensional iterated integral of variables $w_j\in \mathbb{H}$, such that the completion of the Appell function takes the form of an iterated Eichler integral of modular forms. We apply this to the case of the root lattice of the $A_N$ Lie algebra, which is of particular interest because of their appearance in partition functions of topological twisted Yang-Mills theories.

    🤖 AI 总结

    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明了GL_3(Q)的尖点自守表示π的自守周期与其到实二次域E的Arthur-Clozel基变换的周期之间的p进整除性。作为推论，我们建立了π的伴随动机经偶二次特征扭曲后Bloch-Kato猜想所预测的一个整除性。这推广了Tilouine-Urban和Hida在经典模形式情形下的早期工作。我们证明的整除性涉及GL_3(E)的共轭自对偶尖点自守表示的一种新型自守周期，定义在尖点上同调的中间次数而非最高或最低次数。此外，我们证明了GL_3(E)的类似Hida的伴随L值公式，将这些新定义的中间次数周期与通常的最高和最低自守周期联系起来。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove a $p$-adic divisibility between the automorphic periods of a cuspidal automorphic representation $\pi$ of $\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{Q})$ and the periods of its Arthur-Clozel base change to a real quadratic field $E$. As a corollary, we establish a divisibility predicted by the Bloch-Kato conjecture for the adjoint motive of $\pi$ twisted by an even quadratic character. This generalizes earlier works of Tilouine-Urban and Hida in the case of classical modular forms. The divisibility we prove involves a new kind of automorphic periods for conjugate self-dual cuspidal automorphic representations of $\mathrm{GL}_3(E)$, defined within the middle degree of the cuspidal cohomology instead of the top or bottom degrees. Moreover, we prove an \`a la Hida adjoint $L$-value formula for $\mathrm{GL}_3(E)$ that relates these newly defined middle-degree periods to the usual top and bottom automorphic periods.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究有限集A⊂Z^d的几何逆问题，其中A的Fourier L^1范数（或Wiener范数）‖A‖:=∫_{T^d}|∑_{a∈A}e(a·t)|dt关于|A|是次多项式的。特别地，我们证明多种几何现象与小的Fourier L^1范数不相容。我们的主要应用涉及球面Freiman模型。假设‖A‖=|A|^{o(1)}且D⊆A与球面上的格点集S是Freiman同构的；则|D|/|A|必须关于|A|是多项式小的。此外，若|D|/|A|不太小，则S的几乎所有点位于少数球冠中，且几乎所有球冠具有丰富的加法结构。证明使用了研究A的子集间加法结构的新机制，以及Bourgain-Demeter的解耦结果。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study geometric inverse problems for finite sets $A\subset \mathbb{Z}^d$ whose Fourier $L^1$ norm, or Wiener norm, \begin{align*} \|A\|:=\int_{\mathbb{T}^d}\Big|\sum_{a\in A}e(a\cdot t)\Big|dt \end{align*} is subpolynomial in $|A|$. Moreover, if $|D|/|A|$ is not too small, almost all of $S$ lies in a small number of spherical caps, and almost all caps have rich additive structure.     A finite arithmetic form of Robin's inequality and its equivalence to the Riemann hypothesis
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文研究Robin不等式与Riemann假设的等价性。
    • 引入了有限截断版本，其界逐点强于原不等式。
    • 在若干类整数上无条件证明，并证明其等价于Riemann假设。
    • 最小反例必为超丰数，将障碍限制在极值类中。
    💡 引入了Robin不等式的有限截断版本，证明其与Riemann假设等价，并在若干类整数上无条件验证。
    📖 中文摘要

    Robin不等式将Riemann假设重新表述为对除数和函数σ的界。我们引入由n的算术结构内在决定的Robin不等式的有限截断版本：σ(n)/n < e^γ ∑_{j=0}^{ω(n)} (ln ln ln n)^j/j!，n>5040，其中ω(n)表示n的不同素因子个数，γ是Euler-Mascheroni常数。所得界逐点强于Robin不等式，我们在ω(n)≤6的整数、素数阶乘、奇数和无平方因子整数上无条件证明了它。我们还证明该不等式等价于Robin不等式，从而等价于Riemann假设。因此，Riemann假设等价于该截断不等式对所有巨丰数成立。我们进一步证明，若该不等式不成立，其最小反例必为超丰数，从而将任何障碍置于一个严格的极值整数类中。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Robin's inequality reformulates the Riemann hypothesis as a bound on the sum-of-divisors function $\sigma$. We introduce a finite-truncation version of Robin's inequality determined intrinsically by the arithmetic structure of $n$, \[ \frac{\sigma(n)}{n} < e^\gamma \sum_{j=0}^{\omega(n)} \frac{(\ln\ln\ln n)^j}{j!}, \qquad n>5040, \] where $\omega(n)$ denotes the number of distinct prime factors of $n$, and $\gamma$ is the Euler-Mascheroni constant. The resulting bound is pointwise stronger than Robin's inequality and we prove it unconditionally for integers with $\omega(n)\le 6$, primorials, odd integers and square-free integers. We also prove that this inequality is equivalent to Robin's inequality, and hence to the Riemann hypothesis. Consequently, the Riemann hypothesis is equivalent to the truncated inequality holding for all colossally abundant numbers. We further show that if the inequality fails, its minimal counterexample must be a superabundant number, placing any obstruction within a rigid extremal class of integers.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究(3,2)-Raney数模素数p的零游程结构。对于每个素数p，我们确定了零游程长度的从左到右最大值、完整的零游程谱以及0≤n<p^m中非零项的精确数目。有趣的是，这些结果分为三种情形：p=2、p=3和p≥5，且这三种情形的行为非常不同。对于p=2，我们精确刻画了奇数项并确定了从左到右最大值的位置，结果涉及Fibbinary整数、Fibonacci数和Jacobsthal数。对于p=3，我们精确刻画了非零项并确定了它们的剩余类。对于p≥5，零游程由模p的幂的多尺度剩余区间系统控制，由此得到记录值和完整的零游程谱。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the zero-run structure of the $(3,2)$-Raney numbers modulo a prime $p$. Interestingly, these results fall into three cases: $p=2$, $p=3$, and $p\geq5$, and the behaviors in these three cases are very different. For $p=2$, we characterize exactly the odd terms and determine the positions of the left-to-right maxima and the results involve Fibbinary integers, Fibonacci numbers, and Jacobsthal numbers. For $p=3$, we characterize exactly the nonzero terms and determine their residues. For $p\ge 5$, the zero runs are governed by a multiscale system of residue intervals modulo powers of $p$, from which both the record values and the complete zero-run spectrum are obtained.

    • 建立了Eisenstein变换与Jacobi算子散射变换的酉对应，得到Plancherel测度。
    • 推导了矩阵值Maass-Selberg公式，并区分了特征值与散射极点。
    • 为尖点商上的谱理论提供了统一框架。
    💡 在Bruhat-Tits树的算术商上建立了Eisenstein变换与Jacobi算子散射变换的酉对应，并得到Plancherel测度和矩阵值Maass-Selberg公式。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于具有有限多个尖点的Bruhat-Tits树的算术商，我们建立了球面Eisenstein变换（Eisenstein级数由其常数项归一化）与具有有限核心的关联Jacobi算子的散射变换之间的显式酉对应。追踪Haar测度、稳定子权重、高度坐标和尖点宽度，得到了Plancherel测度，并表明绝对连续谱的重数等于尖点数目。从离散Green恒等式，我们推导出Hermitian矩阵iS(θ)^*∂_θS(θ)的矩阵值Maass-Selberg公式，其中S(θ)是散射矩阵。其迹由det S(θ)决定，而完整矩阵保留了额外的尖点到尖点信息。在相应的归一化改变后，通过消去尖点射线得到的有限Schur补与Arends-Peterson-Weich的共振矩阵一致。利用他们的共振计算作为输入，我们区分了完全支撑在有限核心中的特征值与散射矩阵的极点。Nagao和Γ_0(T)商，以及由F_3上椭圆曲线产生的四尖点商，使归一化和矩阵值结论变得明确。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For arithmetic quotients of Bruhat--Tits trees with finitely many cusps, we establish an explicit unitary correspondence between the spherical Eisenstein transform, with the Eisenstein series normalized by their constant terms, and the scattering transform of an associated Jacobi operator with finite core. Tracking the Haar measure, stabilizer weights, height coordinates, and cusp widths yields the Plancherel measure and shows that the absolutely continuous spectrum has multiplicity equal to the number of cusps. From a discrete Green identity we derive a matrix-valued Maass--Selberg formula for the Hermitian matrix $iS(\theta)^*\partial_\theta S(\theta)$, where $S(\theta)$ is the scattering matrix. Its trace is determined by $\det S(\theta)$, while the full matrix retains additional cusp-to-cusp information. After the corresponding change of normalization, the finite Schur complement obtained by eliminating the cusp rays agrees with the resonance matrix of Arends-Peterson-Weich. Using their resonance computations as input, we distinguish eigenvalues supported entirely in the finite core from poles of the scattering matrix. The Nagao and $\Gamma_0(T)$ quotients, together with a four-cusp quotient arising from an elliptic curve over $\mathbb F_3$, make the normalizations and matrix-valued conclusions explicit.

    Support-Primitive Decomposition of Constacyclic Codes over Finite Fields: Coefficients-Based and Roots-Based Descriptions
    🤖 AI 总结

    📖 中文摘要

    设C=(f)是F_q上的λ-常循环码，其中f(X)是X^N-λ的首一因子且常数项非零。我们引入f(X)的支撑周期sp(f)，并定义其支撑本原核心f_sp(X)为满足f(X)=f_sp(X^s)的唯一支撑本原多项式，其中s=sp(f)。我们证明该多项式关系诱导了保持Hamming重量的线性同构C≅C_sp^s，其中C_sp是C的支撑本原核心，并证明该分解是码的内在性质。我们给出支撑周期的两种等价描述：基于系数的和基于根的。在重复根情形，后者由p进结构和定义函数的稳定子决定；在单根情形，它由定义集的最粗多重等差分表示决定。然后我们推导了Hamming距离、重量枚举、覆盖半径和欧几里得对偶的编码理论推论。特别地，单根常循环码的算术Singleton界被等同于其支撑本原核心的经典Singleton界。最后，我们将分解应用于生成多项式可约的循环码，并得到其算术Singleton值的界。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\mathcal C=(f)$ be a $\lambda$-constacyclic code over $\mathbb F_q$, where $f(X)$ is a monic factor of $X^N-\lambda$ with nonzero constant term. We introduce the support period $\operatorname{sp}(f)$ of $f(X)$, and define its support-primitive core $f_{\mathrm{sp}}(X)$ as the unique support-primitive polynomial satisfying $f(X)=f_{\mathrm{sp}}(X^s)$, where $s=\operatorname{sp}(f)$. We show that this polynomial relation induces a Hamming-weight-preserving linear isomorphism $\mathcal C\cong\mathcal C_{\mathrm{sp}}^{\, s}$, where $\mathcal C_{\mathrm{sp}}$ is the support-primitive core of $\mathcal C$, and prove that this decomposition is intrinsic to the code. We give two equivalent descriptions of the support period: a coefficient-based one and a roots-based one. In the repeated-root case, the latter is determined by the $p$-adic structure and the stabilizer of the defining function, while in the simple-root case it is determined by the coarsest multiple equal-difference representation of the defining set. We then derive coding-theoretic consequences for the Hamming distance, weight enumerator, covering radius, and Euclidean duality. In particular, the arithmetic Singleton bound of a simple-root constacyclic code is identified with the classical Singleton bound of its support-primitive core. Finally, we apply the decomposition to cyclic codes with reducible generator polynomials and obtain bounds for their arithmetic Singleton values.

    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究采样具有未知自同态环的随机超奇异椭圆曲线的问题。该问题最近受到广泛关注，因为许多基于同源的密码协议需要此类“安全”曲线来实例化，而现有方法仅在可信设置下实现。我们提出了首个可证明的量子多项式时间算法，以高概率采样此类曲线，其中一个基于Booher等人的算法。一个变体在启发式下以Õ(log^4 p)量子门复杂度运行，在广义Riemann假设下以Õ(log^13 p)运行，并输出一个可证明安全的曲线，假设自同态环问题的平均情形困难性。另一个变体采样具有未知自同态环的均匀O-定向曲线，对于任何虚二次序O，安全性基于向量化问题的困难性。当伴随交互式量子计算验证协议时，我们的算法为CGL哈希函数及相关原语提供了安全实例化。我们的分析依赖于超奇异ℓ-同源图的一个新的谱去局域化结果：我们证明了量子唯一遍历性猜想，并提供了完全特征向量去局域化的数值证据。我们还证明了同源图特征值的一个比Kane、Sharif和Silverberg的量子货币协议所预测的更强的ε-分离性质，从而消除了他们构造中的一个关键启发式假设。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we study the problem of sampling random supersingular elliptic curves with unknown endomorphism rings. This problem has recently gained considerable attention as many isogeny-based cryptographic protocols require such ``secure'' curves for instantation, while existing methods achieve this only in a trusted-setup setting. We present the first provable quantum polynomial-time algorithms for sampling such curves with high probability, one of which is based on an algorithm of Booher et. al. One variant runs heuristically in $\tilde{O}(\log^{4} p)$ quantum gate complexity, and in $\tilde{O}(\log^{13} p)$ under the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis, and outputs a curve that is provably secure assuming average-case hardness of the endomorphism ring problem. Another variant samples uniform $\mathcal{O}$-oriented curves with unknown endomorphism rings, for any imaginary quadratic order $\mathcal O$, with security based on the hardness of Vectorization problem. When accompanied by an interactive quantum computation verification protocol our algorithms provide a secure instantiation of the CGL hash function and related primitives. Our analysis relies on a new spectral delocalization result for supersingular $\ell$-isogeny graphs: we prove the Quantum Unique Ergodicity conjecture and provide numerical evidence for complete eigenvector delocalization. We also prove a stronger $\varepsilon$-separation property for eigenvalues of isogeny graphs than that predicted in the quantum money protocol of Kane, Sharif, and Silverberg, thereby removing a key heuristic assumption in their construction.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究p进约化群G的两个具有相同Levi子群M的抛物子群P,Q的谱Eisenstein级数Eis_{P^∨}, Eis_{Q^∨}之间的谱intertwining算子，受到p进约化群的抛物诱导表示之间的经典intertwining算子的类比启发。特别地，我们构造了满足传递性的归一化（典范）intertwining算子，以及一个非归一化intertwining算子，它是Bruhat-Mackey滤过类似物的有理截面的伴随。此外，归一化和非归一化intertwining算子相差一个L函数的比值，类似于关于经典算子的Langlands猜想（相差单位）。最后，我们证明在任何猜想的范畴局部Langlands对应下，谱intertwining算子对应于经典算子（相差单位）。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study spectral intertwining operators between spectral Eisenstein series $\operatorname{Eis}_{P^\vee}$, $\operatorname{Eis}_{Q^\vee}$ for two parabolic subgroups $P, Q$ of a $p$-adic reductive group $G$ with the same Levi subgroup $M$, inspired by the analogy with the classical intertwining operators between parabolic induced representations of $p$-adic reductive groups. In particular, we construct the normalized (canonical) intertwining operator that satisfies the transitivity and an unnormalized intertwining operator that is adjoint to a rational section of an analog of the Bruhat-Mackey's filtration. Moreover, the normalized and unnormalized intertwining operators differ by a ratio of L-functions, analogously to the Langlands conjecture about classical ones up to units. Finally we prove that the spectral intertwining operators correspond to classical ones up to units under any conjectural categorical local Langlands correspondence.
